if t then local ok, r = pcall(require, t); if ok then _TraD = r end end
if _MutD or _TraD then _dataOk = true end
end)
end
local function calculateGeneration(index, mutation, traits)
ensureSyncData()
if type(AnimalsData) ~= "table" then return 0 end
local info = AnimalsData[index]
if type(info) ~= "table" or not rawget(info, "Generation") then return 0 end
local base = rawget(info, "Generation")
local mult = 1
if mutation and mutation ~= "None" and mutation ~= "" and type(MutationsData) == "table" then
local mInfo = MutationsData[mutation]
if type(mInfo) == "table" and rawget(mInfo, "Modifier") then
mult = mult + rawget(mInfo, "Modifier")
end
end
if type(traits) == "table" and type(TraitsData) == "table" then
for _, tr in ipairs(traits) do
local tInfo = TraitsData[tr]
if type(tInfo) == "table" and rawget(tInfo, "MultiplierModifier") then
mult = mult + rawget(tInfo, "MultiplierModifier")
end
end
end
return math.floor(base * mult)
end
local function _generation(index, mutation, traits)
local AD = _mpsData()
if type(AD) ~= "table" then return 0 end
local info = AD[index]
if type(info) ~= "table" then return 0 end
local base = rawget(info, "Generation")
if not base then return 0 end
_chargerModifs()
local mult = 1
if mutation and mutation ~= "None" and mutation ~= "" and type(_MutD) == "table" then
local mi = _MutD[mutation]
if type(mi) == "table" and rawget(mi, "Modifier") then
mult = mult + rawget(mi, "Modifier")
end
end
if type(traits) == "table" and type(_TraD) == "table" then
for _, tr in ipairs(traits) do
local ti = _TraD[tr]
if type(ti) == "table" and rawget(ti, "MultiplierModifier") then
mult = mult + rawget(ti, "MultiplierModifier")
end
end
end
return math.floor(base * mult)
end
local function getPlotOwner(plot)
local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
if not sign then return nil end
local gui = sign:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SurfaceGui", true)
if not gui then return nil end
local label = gui:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel", true)
if not label then return nil end
local text = label.Text
if not text or text == "" or text:lower():find("empty") then return nil end
local owner = text:match("^(.+)'s Base$")
return owner or text
end
local function _proprietaire(plot)
local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
if not sign then return nil end
local ok, res = pcall(function()
local gui = sign:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SurfaceGui", true)
if not gui then return nil end
local lab = gui:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel", true)
if not lab then return nil end
local txt = lab.Text
if not txt or txt == "" or txt:lower():find("empty") then return nil end
return txt:match("^(.+)'s Base$") or txt
end)
return ok and res or nil
end
local function _fmtGenText(genVal)
genVal = tonumber(genVal) or 0
local s
if NumberUtils and NumberUtils.ToString then
local ok, txt = pcall(function() return NumberUtils:ToString(genVal) end)
if ok and type(txt) == "string" and txt ~= "" then
s = txt
end
end
if not s then s = tostring(genVal) end
return "$" .. s .. "/s"
end
local _BLOCKING_MACHINE_TYPES = { Fuse = true, Duel = true, Trade = true, Crafting = true }
local function _syncOwnerName(owner)
if owner == nil then return nil end
if typeof(owner) == "Instance" and owner:IsA("Player") then return owner.Name end
if typeof(owner) == "Instance" and owner.Name then return owner.Name end
if type(owner) == "string" then return owner end
if type(owner) == "table" and owner.Name then return tostring(owner.Name) end
if type(owner) == "table" and owner.UserId then
local plr = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(owner.UserId)
return plr and plr.Name or nil
end
if type(owner) == "number" then
local plr = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(owner)
return plr and plr.Name or nil
end
return tostring(owner)
end
local function _isMineOwner(ownerName)
if type(ownerName) ~= "string" or ownerName == "" then return false end
local low = ownerName:lower()
local me = LP.Name:lower()
local disp = LP.DisplayName:lower()
if low == me or low == disp then return true end
local fromSign = low:match("^(.+)'s base$")
if fromSign and (fromSign == me or fromSign == disp) then return true end
return false
end
local STEAL_NAME_BLACKLIST = {
octopubase = true,
octopusbase = true,
}
local function _isBlacklistedPet(p)
if not p then return true end
local n = _normName(p.name)
local i = _normName(p.index)
return (n ~= "" and STEAL_NAME_BLACKLIST[n] == true) or (i ~= "" and STEAL_NAME_BLACKLIST[i] == true)
end
local function _plotIsMine(plotRef)
if plotRef == nil then return false end
local plot = plotRef
if type(plotRef) ~= "userdata" then
local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
plot = plots and plots:FindFirstChild(tostring(plotRef))
end
if not plot then return false end
return _isMineOwner(getPlotOwner(plot) or "")
end
local function _isOwnPet(p)
if not p then return true end
if p.mine == true then return true end
if _isMineOwner(tostring(p.owner or "")) then return true end
if p.plot and _plotIsMine(p.plot) then return true end
return false
end
local _petAllowed = function(p)
if not p or p.conveyor then return false end
if _isOwnPet(p) then return false end
if _isBlacklistedPet(p) then return false end
return true
end
local function _isFusingAnimal(animalData)
if type(animalData) ~= "table" then return false end
local m = animalData.Machine
if type(m) ~= "table" then return false end
return _BLOCKING_MACHINE_TYPES[m.Type] == true and m.Active == true
end
local function _extractAnimalList(plotData)
if type(plotData) ~= "table" then return nil end
local animalList = rawget(plotData, "AnimalList") or plotData.AnimalList
if type(animalList) ~= "table" then
animalList = channelGet(plotData, "AnimalList")
end
if type(animalList) ~= "table" then
local ct = rawget(plotData, "CacheTable") or plotData.CacheTable
if type(ct) == "table" then
animalList = rawget(ct, "AnimalList") or ct.AnimalList or ct.Animals
end
end
if type(animalList) ~= "table" then
animalList = rawget(plotData, "Animals") or plotData.Animals
end
if type(animalList) ~= "table" then return nil end
return animalList
end
local _RequestData
local _plotDataCache, _plotDataCacheT = {}, {}
local function getRequestData()
if _RequestData then return _RequestData end
pcall(function()
local pkg = RS:FindFirstChild("Packages")
local sync = pkg and pkg:FindFirstChild("Synchronizer")
_RequestData = sync and sync:FindFirstChild("RequestData")
end)
return _RequestData
end
local function readPlotDataServer(plot)
if not plot then return nil end
local nome = plot.Name
local now = os.clock()
if _plotDataCache[nome] and (now - (_plotDataCacheT[nome] or 0)) < 0.75 then
return _plotDataCache[nome]
end
local rd = getRequestData()
if not rd then return nil end
local ok, data = pcall(function() return rd:InvokeServer(nome) end)
if not ok or type(data) ~= "table" then return nil end
_plotDataCache[nome] = data
_plotDataCacheT[nome] = now
return data
end
_G.JewskiReadPlotDataServer = readPlotDataServer
local function _plotChannelKeys(plot)
local keys, out = {}, {}
local function add(k)
k = type(k) == "string" and k or (k ~= nil and tostring(k) or "")
if k ~= "" and not keys[k] then
keys[k] = true
out[#out + 1] = k
end
end
if type(plot) == "string" then
add(plot)
elseif plot then
local ord = plot:GetAttribute("Order")
if ord ~= nil then add("Plot" .. tostring(ord)) end
add(plot.Name)
end
return out
end
local function _warmPlotChannel(ch)
if not ch then return end
pcall(function()
if type(ch.Get) == "function" then
ch:Get("AnimalList")
ch:Get("Owner")
end
end)
end
local function _readPlotSync(plot)
ensureSyncData()
local ch = getPlotChannel(plot)
if not ch then
for _, key in ipairs(_plotChannelKeys(plot)) do
ch = getPlotChannel(key)
if ch then break end
end
end
if ch then
local animalList = (_G.sProp and _G.sProp(ch, "AnimalList")) or _extractAnimalList(ch)
local owner = (_G.sProp and _G.sProp(ch, "Owner")) or channelGet(ch, "Owner") or rawget(ch, "Owner") or ch.Owner
if type(animalList) == "table" and next(animalList) ~= nil then
return animalList, owner
end
end
return nil, nil
end
local function _findPodiumModel(plot, slotName)
local podiums = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
local podium = podiums and podiums:FindFirstChild(slotName)
if not podium then return nil, nil end
for _, child in ipairs(podium:GetChildren()) do
if child:IsA("Model") and child:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
if not Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(child) then
return child, podium
end
end
end
return nil, podium
end
local _appendPetFromSync = function(plot, owner, moi, now, pets, slot, entry)
if type(entry) == "string" then
entry = { Index = entry }
end
if type(entry) ~= "table" then return end
local index = entry.Index or entry.AnimalIndex or entry.Animal or entry.Name
if type(index) ~= "string" or index == "" then return end
ensureSyncData()
local info = AnimalsData and AnimalsData[index]
if type(info) ~= "table" then return end
if info.Egg == true or info.LuckyBlock == true then return end
if _isFusingAnimal(entry) then return end
local mutation = entry.Mutation or "None"
if mutation == "Yin Yang" then mutation = "YinYang" end
local traits = entry.Traits
local genVal = calculateGeneration(index, mutation, traits)
if genVal <= 0 then
genVal = tonumber(_up9Mps({ Index = index, Mutation = mutation, Traits = traits })) or 0
end
if genVal <= 0 and type(_G.JewskiGen) == "function" then
genVal = tonumber(_G.JewskiGen(index, mutation, traits)) or 0
end
local slotName = tostring(slot)
local displayName = (type(info) == "table" and info.DisplayName) or index
if _isBlacklistedPet({ name = displayName, index = index }) then return end
local pos = getPetPosition(plot, slotName)
local model, podium = _findPodiumModel(plot, slotName)
if not pos and model then
local okp, cf = pcall(function() return model:GetPivot() end)
if okp and cf and cf.Position.Magnitude > 1 then pos = cf.Position end
end
if pos and pos.Magnitude <= 1 then pos = nil end
local prompt
local podiumInst = podium
if not podiumInst then
local podiums = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
podiumInst = podiums and podiums:FindFirstChild(slotName)
end
if podiumInst then
local base = podiumInst:FindFirstChild("Base")
local spawn = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
local att = spawn and spawn:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
local found = att and att:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt")
if found then
local txt = string.lower(tostring(found.ActionText or ""))
if txt:find("steal", 1, true) or txt == "" then
prompt = found
end
end
end
if not pos then return end
local sp = _petSeenT[plot.Name]
if not sp then sp = {}; _petSeenT[plot.Name] = sp end
local seen = sp[slotName]
if not seen or seen.i ~= index then
seen = { t = now, i = index }
sp[slotName] = seen
end
pets[#pets + 1] = {
plot      = plot.Name,
owner     = owner,
slot      = slotName,
name      = displayName,
index     = index,
genValue  = genVal,
genText   = _fmtGenText(genVal),
mps       = genVal,
mutation  = mutation,
traits    = traits,
position  = pos,
uid       = plot.Name .. "_" .. slotName,
model     = model,
podium    = podium,
prompt    = prompt,
firstSeen = seen.t,
mine      = _isMineOwner(owner),
sync      = true,
}
end
local function _scanPlotFromSync(plot, moi, now, pets)
local ch = getPlotChannel(plot)
if ch then
local ownerCh = (_G.sProp and _G.sProp(ch, "Owner")) or channelGet(ch, "Owner")
if _isMineOwner(_syncOwnerName(ownerCh) or "") then return true end
end
local animalList, ownerRaw = _readPlotSync(plot)
if type(animalList) ~= "table" then return false end
local owner = _syncOwnerName(ownerRaw) or getPlotOwner(plot)
if _isMineOwner(owner or "") then return true end
for slot, entry in pairs(animalList) do
_appendPetFromSync(plot, owner, moi, now, pets, slot, entry)
end
return true
end
local function _petStillLive(pet)
if type(pet) ~= "table" or not pet.plot or pet.slot == nil then return false end
local plotName = tostring(pet.plot)
local slotName = tostring(pet.slot)
local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
local plot = plots and plots:FindFirstChild(plotName)
if not plot then return false end
if pet.model and pet.model.Parent then return true end
local ch = getPlotChannel(plotName)
if ch then
local al = _extractAnimalList(ch)
if type(al) == "table" then
local entry = al[slotName] or al[tonumber(slotName)]
if type(entry) ~= "table" then return false end
local idx = entry.Index or entry.AnimalIndex or entry.Animal or entry.Name
if type(idx) ~= "string" or idx == "" then return false end
if pet.index and idx ~= pet.index then return false end
return true
end
end
local podiums = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
local podium = podiums and podiums:FindFirstChild(slotName)
if not podium then return false end
for _, child in ipairs(podium:GetChildren()) do
if child:IsA("Model") and child:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
if not Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(child) then return true end
end
end
return false
end
_G.JewskiPetStillLive = _petStillLive
local _podListCache = {}
local _scanCatalogo
local _SUF = { K = 1e3, M = 1e6, B = 1e9, T = 1e12, Q = 1e15, Qi = 1e18, Sx = 1e21, Sp = 1e24, Oc = 1e27 }
local function _montarCatalogo()
if _scanCatalogo and next(_scanCatalogo) then return _scanCatalogo end
_scanCatalogo = {}
ensureSyncData()
local AD = AnimalsData or _mpsData()
if type(AD) ~= "table" then return _scanCatalogo end
for idx, info in pairs(AD) do
if type(idx) == "string" then
_scanCatalogo[idx:lower()] = idx
if type(info) == "table" and type(info.DisplayName) == "string" then
_scanCatalogo[info.DisplayName:lower()] = idx
end
end
end
return _scanCatalogo
end
local function _mpsDoTexto(model)
if not model then return nil end
local achado
local gui = model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BillboardGui", true) or model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SurfaceGui", true)
local labels = gui and gui:GetDescendants() or nil
if not labels then return nil end
for _, d in ipairs(labels) do
if d:IsA("TextLabel") and type(d.Text) == "string" then
local num, suf = d.Text:match("%$%s*([%d%.]+)%s*(%a*)%s*/s")
if num then
local v = tonumber(num)
if v then
if suf and suf ~= "" then
local m = _SUF[suf] or _SUF[suf:sub(1, 1):upper() .. (suf:sub(2) or "")]
if m then v = v * m end
end
achado = v
break
end
end
end
end
return achado
end
local _podiumsDe = function(plot)
local c = _podListCache[plot.Name]
if c then return c end
local podiums = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
if not podiums then return nil end
local lista = {}
local filhos = podiums:GetChildren()
local numericos = 0
for _, pod in ipairs(filhos) do
if tonumber(pod.Name) then
numericos = numericos + 1
local ok, pp = pcall(function() return pod:GetPivot().Position end)
if ok then lista[#lista + 1] = { nome = pod.Name, pos = pp } end
end
end
if #lista == 0 then return nil end
if #lista == numericos and numericos > 0 then
_podListCache[plot.Name] = lista
end
return lista
end
local _slotDoModel = function(plot, model)
local lista = _podiumsDe(plot)
if not lista then return nil end
local ok, pivo = pcall(function() return model:GetPivot().Position end)
if not ok then return nil end
local melhor, melhorD
for _, pod in ipairs(lista) do
local d = (pod.pos - pivo).Magnitude
if not melhorD or d < melhorD then melhorD, melhor = d, pod.nome end
end
if melhor and melhorD and melhorD <= 8 then return melhor end
return nil
end
local function _stealPromptForSlot(plot, slot)
local podiums = plot and plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
local podium = podiums and podiums:FindFirstChild(tostring(slot))
local base = podium and podium:FindFirstChild("Base")
local spawnp = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
local att = spawnp and spawnp:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
local prompt = att and att:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt")
if prompt then
local txt = string.lower(tostring(prompt.ActionText or ""))
if txt:find("steal", 1, true) or txt == "" then
return prompt, podium
end
end
return nil, podium
end
local _scanPlotRendered = function(plot, owner, now, pets, seen)
seen = seen or {}
local cat = _montarCatalogo()
for _, m in ipairs(plot:GetChildren()) do
if m:IsA("Model") then
local index = cat[m.Name:lower()]
if type(index) == "string" then
local slot = _slotDoModel(plot, m)
local slotName = slot and tostring(slot)
if slotName and not seen[slotName] then
local info = AnimalsData and AnimalsData[index]
if type(info) == "table" and info.Egg ~= true and info.LuckyBlock ~= true then
local mutation = m:GetAttribute("Mutation") or m:GetAttribute("__mutation") or "None"
if mutation == "Yin Yang" then mutation = "YinYang" end
local genVal = calculateGeneration(index, mutation, nil)
if genVal <= 0 then
genVal = tonumber(_generation(index, mutation, nil)) or 0
end
if genVal <= 0 and type(_G.JewskiGen) == "function" then
genVal = tonumber(_G.JewskiGen(index, mutation, nil)) or 0
end
if type(genVal) ~= "number" or genVal <= 0 then
genVal = _mpsDoTexto(m)
end
if type(genVal) == "number" then
local pos = getPetPosition(plot, slotName)
local displayName = info.DisplayName or index
if pos and not _isBlacklistedPet({ name = displayName, index = index }) then
local prompt, podium = _stealPromptForSlot(plot, slotName)
local sp = _petSeenT[plot.Name]
if not sp then sp = {}; _petSeenT[plot.Name] = sp end
local seenT = sp[slotName]
if not seenT or seenT.i ~= index then
seenT = { t = now, i = index }
sp[slotName] = seenT
end
seen[slotName] = true
pets[#pets + 1] = {
plot      = plot.Name,
owner     = owner,
slot      = slotName,
name      = displayName,
index     = index,
genValue  = genVal,
genText   = _fmtGenText(genVal),
mps       = genVal,
mutation  = mutation,
traits    = nil,
position  = pos,
uid       = plot.Name .. "_" .. slotName,
model     = m,
podium    = podium,
prompt    = prompt,
firstSeen = seenT.t,
mine      = _isMineOwner(owner),
sync      = false,
}
end
end
end
end
end
end
end
end
local _genTentado = false
local function _fixGen()
if _genTentado then return end
_genTentado = true
pcall(function()
local f = AnimalsShared and AnimalsShared.GetGeneration
if not f then return end
local esperado = debug.getupvalue(f, 2)
if esperado ~= nil then
debug.setupvalue(f, 1, function() return esperado end)
end
end)
end
local function _ownerDoPlot(plot)
local ch = getPlotChannel(plot)
if ch then
local o = (_G.sProp and _G.sProp(ch, "Owner")) or channelGet(ch, "Owner")
local name = _syncOwnerName(o)
if type(name) == "string" and name ~= "" then
return name, ch
end
end
local txt = getPlotOwner(plot) or _proprietaire(plot)
if type(txt) == "string" and txt ~= "" then
local low = txt:lower()
if not (low:find("sua base", 1, true) or low:find("your base", 1, true) or low:find("^empty")) then
return txt, ch
end
end
return nil, ch
end
local scanAllPets = function(_light)
local now = os.clock()
local boot = _G.__JEWSKI_BOOT_TS and (now - _G.__JEWSKI_BOOT_TS) < 8
local minGap
if boot then
minGap = _light and 0.03 or 0.05
else
minGap = _light and 0.04 or 0.06
end
if not _G.__scanForce and _G.__scanCachePets and _G.__scanCacheAt and (now - _G.__scanCacheAt) < minGap then
return _G.__scanCachePets
end
_G.__scanForce = nil
ensureSyncData()
_fixGen()
local pets = {}
local Plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
if not Plots then return pets end
local pending = 0
for _, plot in ipairs(Plots:GetChildren()) do
local owner, ch = _ownerDoPlot(plot)
if owner == nil then
pending = pending + 1
end
if not _isMineOwner(owner or "") then
local seen = {}
local animalList
if ch then
animalList = (_G.sProp and _G.sProp(ch, "AnimalList")) or _extractAnimalList(ch)
end
if type(animalList) ~= "table" then
animalList = select(1, _readPlotSync(plot))
end
if type(animalList) == "table" then
for slot, entry in pairs(animalList) do
local n = #pets
_appendPetFromSync(plot, owner, LP.Name, now, pets, slot, entry)
if #pets > n then
seen[tostring(slot)] = true
end
end
end
_scanPlotRendered(plot, owner, now, pets, seen)
if _G.__jewskiTEntry == nil and #pets > 0 then
_G.__jewskiTEntry = now
end
end
end
_G.__jewskiScanPending  = pending
_G.__jewskiScanComplete = (pending == 0)
if #pets > 0 then
_G.__jewskiEarlyReady = true
_G.__jewskiEarlyFrames = (_G.__jewskiEarlyFrames or 0) + 1
end
if _G.__jewskiScanComplete and _G.__jewskiTComplete == nil then
_G.__jewskiTComplete = now
end
if #pets > 0 and _G.__jewskiTAnimList == nil then _G.__jewskiTAnimList = now end
if _light then
_G.__scanCachePets = pets
_G.__scanCacheAt = now
return pets
end
for _, p in ipairs(pets) do
p._pri = _priIndexOf(p.name) or (p.index and _priIndexOf(p.index)) or nil
end
if _G.jewskiStealMode == "highest" then
table.sort(pets, function(a, b) return (a.mps or 0) > (b.mps or 0) end)
else
table.sort(pets, function(a, b)
local pa, pb = a._pri, b._pri
if pa and pb then return pa < pb end
if pa then return true end
if pb then return false end
return (a.mps or 0) > (b.mps or 0)
end)
end
_G.__scanCachePets = pets
_G.__scanCacheAt = now
return pets
end
_G.Jewski_ScanAllPets = scanAllPets
_G.Jewski_ScanAllPets = scanAllPets
task.spawn(function()
local t0 = os.clock()
for i = 1, 12 do
_G.__scanForce = true
pcall(scanAllPets, true)
local targetElapsed = (i / 12) * 0.55
while os.clock() - t0 < targetElapsed do
game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Wait()
end
end
_G.__scanForce = true
pcall(scanAllPets, false)
_G.__jewskiScanWarmed = true
end)
local function _bustScanCache()
_plotDataCache = {}
_plotDataCacheT = {}
_chCache = {}
_podListCache = {}
_podiumPos = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
_scanCatalogo = nil
end
local function _bustPlotCache(plotName)
if not plotName then return end
local key = tostring(plotName)
_chCache[key] = nil
_plotDataCache[key] = nil
_plotDataCacheT[key] = nil
_podListCache[key] = nil
end
_G.JewskiBustPlotCache = _bustPlotCache
_G.JewskiBustScanCache = _bustScanCache
_G.__jewskiEarlyFrames = 0
_G.__jewskiEarlyReady  = false
_G.__jewskiRadarOff    = false
local function scanForTP()
return scanAllPets()
end
local function _petMeetsMinGen(p)
local minG = tonumber(_G.JewskiMinGen) or 0
if minG <= 0 or not p then return true end
local m = tonumber(p.mps) or 0
if m <= 0 and p.index then
m = _up9Mps({ Index = p.index, Mutation = p.mutation, Traits = p.traits }) or 0
p.mps = m
end
return m >= minG
end
local function _petBaseGen(p)
if not p then return 0 end
local AD = _mpsData()
local info = type(AD) == "table" and AD[p.index]
if type(info) == "table" then
return tonumber(rawget(info, "Generation")) or tonumber(p.mps) or 0
end
return tonumber(p.mps) or 0
end
local function _fmtMps(n)
n = tonumber(n) or 0
if n >= 1000000 then return string.format("$%.1fM/s", n / 1000000) end
if n >= 1000 then return string.format("$%.1fk/s", n / 1000) end
return "$" .. tostring(math.floor(n + 0.5)) .. "/s"
end
local function _ensurePetGen(p)
if not p then return 0, "$0/s" end
local m = tonumber(p.mps) or 0
if m <= 0 then m = tonumber(p.genValue) or 0 end
if m <= 0 and p.index then
m = tonumber(_up9Mps({ Index = p.index, Mutation = p.mutation, Traits = p.traits })) or 0
end
if m <= 0 and p.index then
m = tonumber(calculateGeneration(p.index, p.mutation, p.traits)) or 0
end
if m > 0 then
p.mps = m
p.genValue = m
p.genText = _fmtGenText(m)
end
local txt = p.genText
if (not txt or txt == "$0/s") and m > 0 then
txt = _fmtGenText(m)
p.genText = txt
end
return m, txt or _fmtMps(m)
end
_G.JewskiEnsurePetGen = _ensurePetGen
local function _buildStealPetList(pets, mode)
if type(pets) ~= "table" then return {} end
mode = tostring(mode or _G.JewskiStealMode or "priority"):lower()
if mode == "highest" then mode = "value" end
local list = {}
for _, p in ipairs(pets) do
if p and _petAllowed(p) then
if (not p.mps or p.mps <= 0) and p.index then
p.mps = _up9Mps({ Index = p.index, Mutation = p.mutation, Traits = p.traits }) or 0
end
if not p.genText and type(_G.JewskiEnsurePetGen) == "function" then
pcall(function()
local m, t = _G.JewskiEnsurePetGen(p)
if t then p.genText = t end
if m and (not p.mps or p.mps <= 0) then p.mps = m end
end)
end
if p.plot and p.slot then
p.uid = tostring(p.plot) .. "_" .. tostring(p.slot)
end
list[#list + 1] = p
end
end
local hrp = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local myPos = hrp and hrp.Position
if mode == "nearest" and myPos then
table.sort(list, function(a, b)
local da = a.position and (a.position - myPos).Magnitude or math.huge
local db = b.position and (b.position - myPos).Magnitude or math.huge
return da < db
end)
elseif mode == "gen" then
table.sort(list, function(a, b) return _petBaseGen(a) > _petBaseGen(b) end)
elseif mode == "value" then
table.sort(list, function(a, b) return (tonumber(a.mps) or 0) > (tonumber(b.mps) or 0) end)
else
table.sort(list, function(a, b)
local pa = _priIndexOf(a.index) or _priIndexOf(a.name)
local pb = _priIndexOf(b.index) or _priIndexOf(b.name)
if pa and pb then
if pa ~= pb then return pa < pb end
elseif pa then
return true
elseif pb then
return false
end
local ma, mb = tonumber(a.mps) or 0, tonumber(b.mps) or 0
if ma ~= mb then return ma > mb end
local fa, fb = tonumber(a.firstSeen) or 0, tonumber(b.firstSeen) or 0
if fa ~= fb and fa > 0 and fb > 0 then return fa < fb end
local ua = tostring(a.plot or "") .. "_" .. tostring(a.slot or "")
local ub = tostring(b.plot or "") .. "_" .. tostring(b.slot or "")
return ua < ub
end)
end
return list
end
_G.JewskiBuildStealPetList = _buildStealPetList
local function _pickPetByMode(pets, mode)
if type(pets) ~= "table" or #pets == 0 then return nil end
local list = _buildStealPetList(pets, mode)
return list[1]
end
local function _petUid(p)
if not p then return nil end
if p.plot and p.slot then
return tostring(p.plot) .. "_" .. tostring(p.slot)
end
if p.uid then
return tostring(p.uid)
end
return nil
end
local function _stealListSig(list, mode)
mode = tostring(mode or _G.JewskiStealMode or "priority"):lower()
if mode == "highest" then mode = "value" end
if type(list) ~= "table" then return mode .. "|" end
local parts = { mode }
for _, p in ipairs(list) do
parts[#parts + 1] = tostring(_petUid(p) or "")
end
return table.concat(parts, "|")
end
local function _mergeStealPetList(existing, scanned, mode)
mode = tostring(mode or _G.JewskiStealMode or "priority"):lower()
if mode == "highest" then mode = "value" end
if type(scanned) ~= "table" or #scanned == 0 then
if type(existing) ~= "table" or #existing == 0 then return {} end
local kept = {}
for _, p in ipairs(existing) do
if _petStillLive(p) then kept[#kept + 1] = p end
end
return _buildStealPetList(kept, mode)
end
if type(existing) ~= "table" or #existing == 0 then
return _buildStealPetList(scanned, mode)
end
local byUid, order = {}, {}
for _, p in ipairs(existing) do
local uid = _petUid(p)
if uid then
byUid[uid] = p
order[#order + 1] = uid
end
end
local scanSet, added, removed = {}, false, false
for _, p in ipairs(scanned) do
if p and _petAllowed(p) and _petStillLive(p) then 
if p.plot and p.slot then
p.uid = tostring(p.plot) .. "_" .. tostring(p.slot)
end
local uid = _petUid(p)
if uid then
scanSet[uid] = true
local old = byUid[uid]
if old then
local fs = old.firstSeen
for k, v in pairs(p) do old[k] = v end
if fs then old.firstSeen = fs end
else
byUid[uid] = p
order[#order + 1] = uid
added = true
end
end
end
end
local newOrder = {}
for _, uid in ipairs(order) do
if scanSet[uid] and byUid[uid] then
newOrder[#newOrder + 1] = uid
elseif byUid[uid] and _petStillLive(byUid[uid]) then
newOrder[#newOrder + 1] = uid
elseif byUid[uid] then
byUid[uid] = nil
removed = true
end
end
local merged = {}
for _, uid in ipairs(newOrder) do
merged[#merged + 1] = byUid[uid]
end
return _buildStealPetList(merged, mode)
end
_G.JewskiMergeStealPetList = _mergeStealPetList
local function get_all_pets()
local out = {}
local cache = _G.JewskiStealPetList
local minGen = tonumber(_G.JewskiMinGen) or 0
local myName = LP.Name
local myDisplay = LP.DisplayName
local pri = _G._stp_priorityCfg
if type(pri) ~= "table" or #pri == 0 then
pri = _G.SHARED_PRIORITY_ITEMS
end
local prioSet = {}
if type(pri) == "table" then
for _, p in ipairs(pri) do
if type(p) == "string" then
prioSet[string.lower(p)] = true
prioSet[_normName(p)] = true
end
end
end
if type(cache) == "table" then
for _, a in ipairs(cache) do
if a and (tonumber(a.mps) or tonumber(a.genValue) or 0) >= 1 then
local owner = tostring(a.owner or "")
if _petAllowed(a) then
local gen = tonumber(a.mps) or tonumber(a.genValue) or 0
local isBrainrot = gen >= 10000000
local isPriority = (a.name and prioSet[string.lower(tostring(a.name))])
or (a.index and prioSet[string.lower(tostring(a.index))])
or prioSet[_normName(a.name)]
or prioSet[_normName(a.index)]
if minGen > 0 and gen < minGen and not isBrainrot and not isPriority then
continue
end
out[#out + 1] = {
name = a.name,
petName = a.name,
index = a.index,
mpsText = a.genText or a.mpsText,
mpsValue = gen,
mps = gen,
genValue = gen,
owner = a.owner,
plot = a.plot,
slot = a.slot,
uid = a.uid or (a.plot and a.slot and (tostring(a.plot) .. "_" .. tostring(a.slot))),
mutation = a.mutation,
position = a.position,
model = a.model,
animalData = a,
}
end
end
end
end
table.sort(out, function(a, b) return (a.mpsValue or 0) > (b.mpsValue or 0) end)
return out
end
_G.JewskiGetAllPets = get_all_pets
_G.JewskiGetAllPets = get_all_pets
local function _priDbg(key, ...)
if tostring(_G.JewskiStealMode or ""):lower() ~= "priority" then
return
end
local now = os.clock()
local last = _G.__JewskiPriDbgTimes
if type(last) ~= "table" then
last = {}
_G.__JewskiPriDbgTimes = last
end
if (now - (tonumber(last[key]) or 0)) < 2 then
return
end
last[key] = now
end
local function _promptPetUid(prompt)
if not prompt or not prompt.Parent then return nil end
local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
if not plots then return nil end
local plotModel = prompt:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("Model")
while plotModel and plotModel.Parent and plotModel.Parent ~= plots do
plotModel = plotModel.Parent
end
if not plotModel or plotModel.Parent ~= plots then return nil end
local podiums = plotModel:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
if not podiums then return nil end
local node = prompt
while node and node ~= podiums do
if node.Parent == podiums then
return plotModel.Name .. "_" .. tostring(node.Name), plotModel.Name, tostring(node.Name)
end
node = node.Parent
end
return nil
end
local function _findPetByUid(pets, uid)
if type(pets) ~= "table" or type(uid) ~= "string" or uid == "" then return nil end
uid = tostring(uid)
for _, p in ipairs(pets) do
local a = _petUid(p)
local b = (p.plot ~= nil and p.slot ~= nil) and (tostring(p.plot) .. "_" .. tostring(p.slot)) or nil
if a == uid or b == uid or tostring(p.uid or "") == uid then
return p
end
end
return nil
end
local function _findTPSyncedPet(pets)
local uid = _G.jewskiStealTargetUID
if type(uid) ~= "string" or uid == "" then return nil end
for _, p in ipairs(pets) do
if _petUid(p) == uid then return p end
end
return nil
end
local function _clearTPSync()
_G.JewskiTPSyncActive = false
_G.JewskiStealTargetUID = nil
_G.jewskiTPSyncActive = false
_G.jewskiStealTargetUID = nil
end
_G.JewskiClearTPSync = _clearTPSync
local function _selectedStealUid()
local man = _G.JewskiManualStealUID
if type(man) == "string" and man ~= "" then return man end
local sel = _G.JewskiStealTarget or _G.JewskiSelectedPetData
local uid = sel and _petUid(sel)
if type(uid) == "string" and uid ~= "" then return uid end
uid = _G.JewskiStealTargetUID
if type(uid) == "string" and uid ~= "" then return uid end
return nil
end
local function _resolveTPPet(pets)
local function _ok(p)
if not p then return false end
if type(_petAllowed) == "function" and not _petAllowed(p) then return false end
if _G.__JEWSKI_TpOnLoadFilterActive then
local pri = false
if type(_petIsPriority) == "function" then pri = _petIsPriority(p) end
if not pri and type(_priIndexOf) == "function" then
pri = (_priIndexOf(p.index) or _priIndexOf(p.name)) ~= nil
end
if not pri then
local minG = tonumber(_G.JewskiMinGen) or 20000000
if minG <= 0 then minG = 20000000 end
local m = tonumber(p.mps) or tonumber(p.genValue) or 0
if m < minG then return false end
end
end
return true
end
local pet = _findPetByUid(pets, _selectedStealUid())
if pet and _ok(pet) then return pet end
if _G.__JEWSKI_TpOnLoadFilterActive then
if type(pickTPWinner) == "function" then
local w = pickTPWinner(pets)
if w and _ok(w) then return w end
end
if type(pets) == "table" then
local minG = tonumber(_G.JewskiMinGen) or 20000000
if minG <= 0 then minG = 20000000 end
local best, bestM = nil, -1
for _, p in ipairs(pets) do
if _ok(p) then
local m = tonumber(p.mps) or tonumber(p.genValue) or 0
if m > bestM then best, bestM = p, m end
end
end
if best then return best end
end
return nil
end
local picked = _pickPetByMode(pets, _G.JewskiStealMode or "priority")
if picked and _ok(picked) then return picked end
return picked
end
local function _stealPetReady(pet)
if type(pet) ~= "table" then return false end
if not _petAllowed(pet) then return false end
if not pet.position then return false end
if not _petUid(pet) then return false end
local n = pet.name or pet.index
return type(n) == "string" and n ~= ""
end
local function _findStealTarget(pets)
if not _G.jewskiTPSyncActive then return nil end
return _findTPSyncedPet(pets)
end

local UPPER_Y_THRESHOLD = 7
local TALL_PETS = { ["La Secret Combinasion"]=true, ["La Jolly Grande"]=true }
local TALL_OFFSET = 3
local BASES_LOW = {
[1] = Vector3.new(-476.52, -2, 220.94090270996094),
[2] = Vector3.new(-476.52, -2, 113.77315521240234),
[3] = Vector3.new(-476.52, -2, 6.178487777709961),
[4] = Vector3.new(-476.52, -2, -101.07275390625),
[5] = Vector3.new(-342.66, -2, 221.44737243652344),
[6] = Vector3.new(-342.66, -2, 113.41409301757812),
[7] = Vector3.new(-342.66, -2, 6.249461650848389),
[8] = Vector3.new(-342.66, -2, -99.73458862304688),
}
local BASES_HIGH = {
[1] = Vector3.new(-479.51, 18, 220.94090270996094),
[2] = Vector3.new(-479.51, 18, 113.77315521240234),
[3] = Vector3.new(-479.51, 18, 6.178487777709961),
[4] = Vector3.new(-479.51, 18, -101.07275390625),
[5] = Vector3.new(-339.48, 18, 221.44737243652344),
[6] = Vector3.new(-339.48, 18, 113.41409301757812),
[7] = Vector3.new(-339.48, 18, 6.249461650848389),
[8] = Vector3.new(-339.48, 18, -99.73458862304688),
}
local FRONT_Y_LOW   = -3.048217
local FRONT_Y_HIGH  = 16.850713
local COLUMN_SPLIT_X = -410
local FRONT_Z_CLAMP  = 18
local SIDE_NEAR_Z    = 45
local function getClosestBaseIdx(pos)
local closest, dist = 1, math.huge
for i = 1, 8 do
local b = BASES_LOW[i]
local d = (pos.X - b.X)^2 + (pos.Z - b.Z)^2
if d < dist then dist = d; closest = i end
end
return closest
end
local function buildFrontCandidate(idx, isUpper, playerZ)
local base = isUpper and BASES_HIGH[idx] or BASES_LOW[idx]
local frontY = isUpper and FRONT_Y_HIGH or FRONT_Y_LOW
local frontZ = math.clamp(playerZ - base.Z, -FRONT_Z_CLAMP, FRONT_Z_CLAMP) + base.Z
local coord = Vector3.new(base.X, frontY, frontZ)
local faceDir = (idx <= 4) and Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0) or Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
return coord, faceDir
end
local function plotSides(coordTable, idx)
local base = BASES_LOW[idx]
local isWest = idx <= 4
local out = {}
for _, coords in pairs(coordTable) do
for _, data in ipairs(coords) do
if ((data.coord.X < COLUMN_SPLIT_X) == isWest)
and math.abs(data.coord.Z - base.Z) < SIDE_NEAR_Z then
out[#out + 1] = data
end
end
end
return out
end
local function _floor1LaserSolid(plotName)
local solid = false
pcall(function()
local Plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
local plot = Plots and Plots:FindFirstChild(plotName)
if not plot then return end
local folder = plot:FindFirstChild("Laser")
local parts = folder and folder:GetChildren() or plot:GetChildren()
for _, d in ipairs(parts) do
if d:IsA("BasePart") and (d.Name == "LaserHitbox" or d.Name == "Laser")
and d.CanCollide and d.Position.Y <= 9 then
solid = true
break
end
end
end)
return solid
end
local function isPlotUnlocked(plotName)
local ok, res = pcall(function()
local channel = getPlotChannel(plotName)
if not channel then return false end
if channelGet(channel, "BlockEndTimeFirstFloor") ~= nil then return false end
return not _floor1LaserSolid(plotName)
end)
return ok and (res == true)
end
local function findClosest(petPos, coordTable)
local best, bestKey, bestDist = nil, nil, math.huge
for skyKey, coords in pairs(coordTable) do
for _, data in ipairs(coords) do
local c = data.coord
local d = math.sqrt((petPos.X - c.X)^2 + (petPos.Z - c.Z)^2)
if d < bestDist then bestDist = d; best = data; bestKey = skyKey end
end
end
return best, bestKey
end
local SPEED = 125
local ARRIVE = 3
local _STRIP_OK = (type(getconnections) == "function")
local function _climbCap()
local v = math.clamp(tonumber(_G.jewskiClimb) or 400, 100, 800)
if not _STRIP_OK then v = math.min(v, tonumber(_G.jewskiClimbSafe) or 250) end
return v
end
local function vZero(hrp)
if hrp then hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero; hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero end
end
local function velMoveThrough(hrp, waypoints, speedOverride, allowJump, quickStart)
if type(_G.__VPRIV_velMoveThrough) == "function" then
return _G.__VPRIV_velMoveThrough(hrp, waypoints, speedOverride, allowJump, quickStart)
end
if not hrp or not waypoints or #waypoints == 0 then return end
for _, wp in ipairs(waypoints) do
if typeof(wp) == "Vector3" and hrp and hrp.Parent then
pcall(function()
local _, y = hrp.CFrame:ToEulerAnglesYXZ()
hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(wp) * CFrame.Angles(0, y, 0)
hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
end)
for _ = 1, 2 do game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Wait() end
end
end
end
_G.Jewski_ClearWide = _G.Jewski_ClearWide or function(a, b) return true end
local computeRoute, _len
function _len(pts)
local s, prev = 0, pts and pts[1]
if not pts then return 0 end
for k = 2, #pts do s = s + (pts[k] - prev).Magnitude; prev = pts[k] end
return s
end
function computeRoute(fromPos, toPos, facingDir, maxLift, preferCrest)
if type(_G.__VPRIV_computeRoute) == "function" then
return _G.__VPRIV_computeRoute(fromPos, toPos, facingDir, maxLift, preferCrest)
end
if not toPos then return { fromPos } end
return { toPos }
end
local _Stats = game:GetService("Stats")
local function _pingMs()
local ok, p = pcall(function() return LP:GetNetworkPing() * 1000 end)
if ok and type(p) == "number" and p > 0 then return p end
local ok2, p2 = pcall(function()
return _Stats.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue()
end)
if ok2 and type(p2) == "number" and p2 > 0 then return p2 end
return 0
end
local function _pingAdjustSpeed(spd)
local thresh = tonumber(_G.jewskiPingThresh) or 170
local capped = tonumber(_G.jewskiHighPingSpeed) or 400
if _pingMs() >= thresh and spd > capped then return capped end
return spd
end
local function _inVoid(hrp)
if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then return true end
local voidY = tonumber(_G.jewskiVoidY) or -50
return hrp.Position.Y < voidY
end
local function _waitOutOfVoid(timeout)
local t0 = os.clock()
local good = 0
while os.clock() - t0 < (timeout or 12) do
if _G.jewskiTPStop then return false end
local char = LP.Character
local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if hrp and hrp.Parent and not _inVoid(hrp) and math.abs(hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y) < 12 then
good += 1
if good >= 4 then return true end
else
good = 0
end
RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
end
return false
end
local isTeleporting = false; _G.JewskiIsTeleporting = false
local _goBrGen = 0
local _cloneTP    = false
local _cloneFired = false
local _lastTPOk   = false
local function endTP()
_G.isTeleporting = false
_G.Jewski_Step("TP: termine")
isTeleporting = false; _G.JewskiIsTeleporting = false
_cloneTP = false
_cloneFired = false
_G.JewskiCloneTP = false
_G.JewskiCloneFired = false
if type(_G.Jewski_EnsureAlwaysOpenUIs) == "function" then
task.defer(_G.Jewski_EnsureAlwaysOpenUIs)
end
end
local function _clearTpGoState()
isTeleporting = false
_G.JewskiIsTeleporting = false
_G.jewskiIsTeleporting = false
_G.jewskiTPStop = false
_G.JewskiTPStop = false
_cloneTP = false
_cloneFired = false
_G.JewskiCloneTP = false
_G.JewskiCloneFired = false
_G.jewskiStealHold = false
_goBrGen = _goBrGen + 1
end
_G.JewskiClearTpGoState = _clearTpGoState
LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
task.defer(_clearTpGoState)
end)
do
local function killAnimate(char)
if not char or _G.jewskiNoAnim == false then return end
task.spawn(function()
local a = char:FindFirstChild("Animate") or char:WaitForChild("Animate", 5)
if a then pcall(function() a.Disabled = true end) end
local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
local anim = hum and hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
if anim then
pcall(function()
for _, t in ipairs(anim:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do t:Stop(0) end
end)
end
end)
end
if LP.Character then killAnimate(LP.Character) end
LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(killAnimate)
end
_G.JewskiInstantClone = function()
if _G.isCloning and (not _G._cloneLockAt or (os.clock() - _G._cloneLockAt) > 1.2) then
_G.isCloning = false
end
if _G.isCloning then return false end
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local LP = Players.LocalPlayer
local c = LP.Character
if not c then return false end
local h = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not h then return false end
local bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
local cl = (bp and bp:FindFirstChild("Quantum Cloner")) or c:FindFirstChild("Quantum Cloner")
if not cl then
local t0 = os.clock()
while os.clock() - t0 < 0.35 and not cl do
task.wait(0.05)
c = LP.Character or c
bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
cl = (bp and bp:FindFirstChild("Quantum Cloner")) or (c and c:FindFirstChild("Quantum Cloner"))
end
end
if not cl then
warn("[JEWSKI] Quantum Cloner not found")
return false
end
pcall(function() h:UnequipTools() end)
task.wait()
if cl.Parent ~= c then
pcall(function() h:EquipTool(cl) end)
task.wait()
end
local pg = LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") or LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 5)
local tf = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("ToolsFrames")
local qc = tf and tf:FindFirstChild("QuantumCloner")
local tb = qc and qc:FindFirstChild("TeleportToClone")
if not tb then
pcall(function() cl:Activate() end)
return true
end
_G.isCloning = true
_G._cloneLockAt = os.clock()
pcall(function() cl:Activate() end)
task.wait(0.05)
pcall(function() tb.Visible = true end)
pcall(function() if firesignal then firesignal(tb.MouseButton1Click) end end)
pcall(function() if firesignal then firesignal(tb.MouseButton1Up) end end)
pcall(function() if firesignal then firesignal(tb.Activated) end end)
pcall(function()
local VIM = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
local abs = tb.AbsolutePosition
local sz = tb.AbsoluteSize
local x = abs.X + sz.X / 2
local y = abs.Y + sz.Y / 2
VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(x, y, 0, true, game, 0)
VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(x, y, 0, false, game, 0)
end)
task.delay(0.15, function() _G.isCloning = false end)
task.wait(0.08)
return true
end
_G.JewskiInstantClone = _G.JewskiInstantClone
_G.instantClone = _G.JewskiInstantClone
instantClone = _G.JewskiInstantClone
local function doClone()
_G.Jewski_Step("clone: lance")
if type(_G.JewskiInstantClone) ~= "function" then
_G.Jewski_Step("clone: echec", "JewskiInstantClone indisponible")
return false
end
local ok, err = pcall(_G.JewskiInstantClone)
if not ok then
_G.Jewski_Step("clone: echec", tostring(err))
return false
end
_G.Jewski_Step("clone: JewskiInstantClone")
return true
end
local function makeOneWay(plat)
if not plat then return end
local rsConn
local lastY = nil
local _platHrp = nil
rsConn = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
if not plat or not plat.Parent then
if rsConn then rsConn:Disconnect() end
return
end
if not (_platHrp and _platHrp.Parent) then
local char = LP.Character
_platHrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end
local hrp = _platHrp
if hrp then
local currentY = hrp.Position.Y
if not lastY then lastY = currentY end
local deltaY = currentY - lastY
local isMovingUp = (hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y > 1) or (deltaY > 0.01 and deltaY < 5)
local want = (not isMovingUp) and (currentY > plat.Position.Y + 0.1)
if plat.CanCollide ~= want then plat.CanCollide = want end
lastY = currentY
end
end)
end

do
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local LP = Players.LocalPlayer
    local POTION = "Body Swap Potion"

    local carry = { active = false, pos = nil, slot = nil, name = nil, gen = 0, t = 0 }
    local lastGriefAt = 0
    local wasRag = false
    local wasStealing = false

    local function notify(title, body, color, extra)
        task.defer(function()
            pcall(function()
                local pg = LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
                if not pg then return end
                local old = pg:FindFirstChild("JewskiVPrivNotify")
                if old then old:Destroy() end
                local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
                sg.Name = "JewskiVPrivNotify"
                sg.ResetOnSpawn = false
                sg.IgnoreGuiInset = true
                sg.DisplayOrder = 250
                sg.Parent = pg
                local f = Instance.new("Frame", sg)
                f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(420, 130)
                f.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -210, 0, 48)
                f.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(12, 12, 18)
                f.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
                f.BorderSizePixel = 0
                Instance.new("UICorner", f).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 14)
                local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", f)
                stroke.Color = color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70)
                stroke.Thickness = 2
                stroke.Transparency = 0.15
                local bar = Instance.new("Frame", f)
                bar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 6)
                bar.BackgroundColor3 = color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70)
                bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
                Instance.new("UICorner", bar).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 14)
                local t = Instance.new("TextLabel", f)
                t.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 28)
                t.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14)
                t.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                t.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                t.TextSize = 18
                t.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                t.TextColor3 = color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 110, 110)
                t.Text = tostring(title or "GRIEFED")
                local b = Instance.new("TextLabel", f)
                b.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 28)
                b.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 46)
                b.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                b.TextSize = 16
                b.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                b.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 250)
                b.Text = tostring(body or "")
                local e = Instance.new("TextLabel", f)
                e.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 36)
                e.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 78)
                e.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                e.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
                e.TextSize = 14
                e.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                e.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
                e.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 185, 200)
                e.TextWrapped = true
                e.Text = tostring(extra or "")
                task.delay(5.5, function() if sg.Parent then sg:Destroy() end end)
            end)
        end)
    end

    local function shortGen(n)
        n = tonumber(n) or 0
        if n >= 1e9 then return string.format("%.1fB", n / 1e9) end
        if n >= 1e6 then return string.format("%.1fM", n / 1e6) end
        if n >= 1e3 then return string.format("%.1fK", n / 1e3) end
        return tostring(math.floor(n))
    end

    local function nearestThreat(maxD)
        maxD = maxD or 50
        local hrp = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return nil, nil, nil end
        local best, bestD, bestTool = nil, maxD, nil
        for _, pl in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if pl ~= LP and pl.Character then
                local p = pl.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if p then
                    local d = (p.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                    if d < bestD then
                        local tool
                        for _, o in ipairs(pl.Character:GetChildren()) do
                            if o:IsA("Tool") then tool = o.Name break end
                        end
                        bestD, best, bestTool = d, pl, tool
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return best, bestTool, bestD
    end

    local function isRagdolled()
        local char = LP.Character
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum then return false end
        local st = hum:GetState()
        return st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
            or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
            or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
    end

    local function hasBoogieEffect(char)
        if not char then return false end
        local hit = false
        pcall(function()
            for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                local n = string.lower(tostring(d.Name or ""))
                if n:find("boogiebomb", 1, true) or n:find("boogie_bomb", 1, true)
                    or n:find("boogie bomb", 1, true) or n == "boogie" then
                    hit = true
                    break
                end
            end
        end)
        return hit
    end

    local function nearestBoogieHolder(maxD)
        maxD = maxD or 55
        local hrp = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return nil, nil end
        local best, bestD = nil, maxD
        for _, pl in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if pl ~= LP and pl.Character then
                local tool = pl.Character:FindFirstChild("Boogie Bomb")
                if tool then
                    local p = pl.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if p then
                        local d = (p.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                        if d < bestD then bestD, best = d, pl end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return best, bestD
    end

    local function snapshotCarry()
        local pet = _G.JewskiStealTarget or _G.JewskiSelectedPetData or _G.__jewskiChosenPet or _G.JewskiLastStealPet
        if type(pet) == "table" then
            local pos = pet.position or pet.Position or pet.pos
            if typeof(pos) == "Vector3" then carry.pos = pos end
            carry.slot = pet.slot or pet.Slot or carry.slot
            carry.name = pet.name or pet.Name or pet.animal or carry.name
            carry.gen = tonumber(pet.mps or pet.mpsValue or pet.genValue or pet.gen) or carry.gen
        end
        local hrp = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if hrp then
            carry.pos = carry.pos or hrp.Position
        end
        carry.active = true
        carry.t = os.clock()
    end

    pcall(function()
        LP:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function()
            if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then
                snapshotCarry()
            end
        end)
    end)

    local function reportGrief(kind, who, tool)
        if _G.JewskiGriefDetector == false then return end
        local now = os.clock()
        if now - lastGriefAt < 1.5 then return end
        lastGriefAt = now
        local by = who and who.Name or "Unknown"
        local uid = who and tostring(who.UserId) or ""
        local gear = tool and tostring(tool) or "Unknown gear"
        local lost = "None"
        if carry.active and carry.name then
            lost = tostring(carry.name)
            if carry.gen and carry.gen > 0 then
                lost = lost .. "  ·  " .. shortGen(carry.gen) .. "/s"
            end
        end
        local title = "GRIEFED - " .. tostring(kind or "Hit")
        local body = "Who:  " .. by .. (uid ~= "" and ("  (id " .. uid .. ")") or "")
        local extra = "Gear:  " .. gear .. "\nLost:  " .. lost
        notify(title, body, Color3.fromRGB(255, 75, 75), extra)
    end

    local _backBusy = false
    local _backLast = 0
    local function goBackToPet(reason)
        if _G.JewskiBackToPetOnHit == false then return end
        if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then return end 
        if typeof(carry.pos) ~= "Vector3" then return end
        if (os.clock() - (carry.t or 0)) > 14 then return end 
        if _backBusy then return end
        if (os.clock() - _backLast) < 4.5 then return end 

        local gtb = _G.Jewski_GoToBrainrot or _G.__VPRIV_goToBrainrot or _G.JewskiGoToBrainrot
        if type(gtb) ~= "function" then return end

        _backBusy = true
        _backLast = os.clock()
        local pos, slot = carry.pos, carry.slot

        task.spawn(function()
            local t0 = os.clock()
            while os.clock() - t0 < 2.0 do
                local char = LP.Character
                local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if hum and hrp and hum.Health > 0 then
                    local st = hum:GetState()
                    if st ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
                        and st ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
                        and st ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
                        and st ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp then
                        break
                    end
                    pcall(function()
                        hum.PlatformStand = false
                        hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
                    end)
                end
                task.wait(0.12)
            end
            task.wait(0.45) 

            if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then
                _backBusy = false
                return
            end
            if typeof(pos) ~= "Vector3" then
                _backBusy = false
                return
            end

            local oldGo = _G.JewskiGoSpeed
            local oldBr = _G.JewskiBrainrotSpeed
            local oldClose = _G.JewskiCloseSpeed
            local oldVel = _G.TPVelocity
            pcall(function()
                local go = tonumber(oldGo) or 200
                local br = tonumber(oldBr) or 200
                local cl = tonumber(oldClose) or 80
                local vel = tonumber(oldVel) or 400
                _G.JewskiGoSpeed = math.clamp(go * 0.45, 60, 140)
                _G.JewskiBrainrotSpeed = math.clamp(br * 0.45, 60, 140)
                _G.JewskiCloseSpeed = math.clamp(cl * 0.6, 30, 70)
                _G.TPVelocity = math.clamp(vel * 0.5, 120, 220)
            end)

            pcall(gtb, pos, slot)

            task.delay(2.8, function()
                pcall(function()
                    if oldGo ~= nil then _G.JewskiGoSpeed = oldGo end
                    if oldBr ~= nil then _G.JewskiBrainrotSpeed = oldBr end
                    if oldClose ~= nil then _G.JewskiCloseSpeed = oldClose end
                    if oldVel ~= nil then _G.TPVelocity = oldVel end
                end)
                _backBusy = false
            end)
        end)
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        local hitCd = {}
        local localJustFired = false
        local prevH, prevL = nil, nil
        local function getHRP(pl)
            local c = pl and pl.Character
            return c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        end
        local function hookLocalPotion()
            local char = LP.Character
            if not char then return end
            local function hook(p)
                pcall(function()
                    p.Activated:Connect(function()
                        localJustFired = true
                        task.delay(1.2, function() localJustFired = false end)
                    end)
                end)
            end
            local ex = char:FindFirstChild(POTION)
            if ex then hook(ex) end
            char.ChildAdded:Connect(function(ch)
                if ch.Name == POTION then hook(ch) end
            end)
        end
        pcall(function()
            LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
                task.wait(0.15)
                hookLocalPotion()
                prevH, prevL = nil, nil
            end)
        end)
        pcall(hookLocalPotion)

        local function swapBack(target)
            pcall(function()
                if _G.JewskiAntiBodySwap == false and _G.AntiBodySwapEnabled == false then return end
                local char = LP.Character
                local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if not hum then return end
                local bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
                local potion = (char and char:FindFirstChild(POTION))
                    or (bp and bp:FindFirstChild(POTION))
                if not potion then return end
                pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(potion) end)
                task.wait(0.04)
                local tHRP = getHRP(target)
                local lHRP = getHRP(LP)
                if tHRP and lHRP then
                    pcall(function()
                        lHRP.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(lHRP.Position, tHRP.Position)
                    end)
                end
                pcall(function() potion:Activate() end)
                pcall(function()
                    if firesignal and potion.Activated then
                    end
                end)
                pcall(function()
                    local VIM = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
                    local pg = LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
                    if VIM and lHRP then
                    end
                end)
            end)
        end

        local _absSkip = 0
        RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            _absSkip = _absSkip + 1
            if _absSkip < 6 then return end
            _absSkip = 0
            if _G.JewskiAntiBodySwap == false and _G.AntiBodySwapEnabled == false then
                prevH, prevL = nil, nil
                return
            end
            local holder
            for _, pl in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if pl ~= LP and pl.Character and pl.Character:FindFirstChild(POTION) then
                    holder = pl
                    break
                end
            end
            if not holder then
                prevH, prevL = nil, nil
                return
            end
            local cd = hitCd[holder.UserId]
            if cd and (tick() - cd) < 8 then return end
            local hHRP, lHRP = getHRP(holder), getHRP(LP)
            if not hHRP or not lHRP then return end
            local cH, cL = hHRP.Position, lHRP.Position
            if prevH and prevL then
                local holderJump = (cH - prevH).Magnitude
                local holderAtMyOld = (cH - prevL).Magnitude < 6
                local iMoved = (cL - prevL).Magnitude > 2.5
                if holderJump > 2.5 and holderAtMyOld and iMoved and not localJustFired then
                    hitCd[holder.UserId] = tick()
                    reportGrief("Body Swap", holder, POTION)
                    task.spawn(function() swapBack(holder) end)
                    if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true or carry.active then
                        snapshotCarry()
                        task.delay(0.35, goBackToPet)
                    end
                end
            end
            prevH, prevL = cH, cL
        end)
    end)

    local _abLast = 0
    local function antiBoogieOnce()
        if _G.JewskiAntiBoogie == false then return end
        local char = LP.Character
        if not char then return end
        pcall(function()
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

            if hum then
                for _, t in ipairs(hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                    local an = string.lower(tostring(
                        t.Name or (t.Animation and t.Animation.Name) or ""
                    ))
                    if an:find("boogie", 1, true) or an:find("dance", 1, true)
                        or an:find("emote", 1, true) then
                        pcall(function() t:Stop(0) end)
                        pcall(function() t:Destroy() end)
                    end
                end
                pcall(function()
                    hum.PlatformStand = false
                    hum.AutoRotate = true
                    if hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
                        or hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
                        or hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then
                        hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
                    end
                end)
            end

            for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                local n = string.lower(tostring(d.Name or ""))
                local isBoogie = n:find("boogie", 1, true) or n:find("dance", 1, true)
                if d:IsA("Sound") then
                    if isBoogie or (d.Playing and isBoogie) then
                        pcall(function() d.Volume = 0 end)
                        pcall(function() d:Stop() end)
                        pcall(function() d:Destroy() end)
                    elseif isBoogie then
                        pcall(function() d.Volume = 0; d:Stop() end)
                    end
                elseif isBoogie then
                    if d:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or d:IsA("Highlight")
                        or d:IsA("Beam") or d:IsA("Trail")
                        or d:IsA("Fire") or d:IsA("Smoke")
                        or d:IsA("Sparkles") or d:IsA("PointLight") then
                        pcall(function() d.Enabled = false end)
                        pcall(function() d:Destroy() end)
                    elseif d:IsA("Animation") or d:IsA("AnimationController") then
                    end
                end
            end

            pcall(function()
                if not hrp then return end
                for _, d in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                    if d:IsA("Sound") then
                        local n = string.lower(tostring(d.Name or ""))
                        if n:find("boogie", 1, true) or n:find("dance", 1, true) then
                            local parent = d.Parent
                            local pos
                            if parent and parent:IsA("BasePart") then
                                pos = parent.Position
                            end
                            if pos and (pos - hrp.Position).Magnitude < 80 then
                                pcall(function() d.Volume = 0 end)
                                pcall(function() d:Stop() end)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)

            pcall(function()
                local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
                if cam and cam.CameraType ~= Enum.CameraType.Custom then
                end
                local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
                for _, fx in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
                    local n = string.lower(tostring(fx.Name or ""))
                    if n:find("boogie", 1, true) or n:find("dance", 1, true) then
                        pcall(function() fx.Enabled = false end)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end)
    end

    do
        local _abAcc = 0
        local _charHooked = nil
        local function hookChar(char)
            if not char or _charHooked == char then return end
            _charHooked = char
            char.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
                if _G.JewskiAntiBoogie == false then return end
                local n = string.lower(tostring(d.Name or ""))
                if not (n:find("boogie", 1, true) or n:find("dance", 1, true)) then return end
                if d:IsA("Sound") then
                    pcall(function() d.Volume = 0 end)
                    pcall(function() d:Stop() end)
                    task.defer(function() pcall(function() d:Destroy() end) end)
                elseif d:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or d:IsA("Highlight") or d:IsA("Trail") or d:IsA("Beam") then
                    pcall(function() d.Enabled = false end)
                    task.defer(function() pcall(function() d:Destroy() end) end)
                end
            end)
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 3)
            if hum then
                hum.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
                    if _G.JewskiAntiBoogie == false then return end
                    local an = string.lower(tostring(
                        track.Name or (track.Animation and track.Animation.Name) or ""
                    ))
                    if an:find("boogie", 1, true) or an:find("dance", 1, true) then
                        pcall(function() track:Stop(0) end)
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
        pcall(function()
            if LP.Character then hookChar(LP.Character) end
            LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c)
                task.wait(0.1)
                hookChar(c)
            end)
        end)
        RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
            if _G.JewskiAntiBoogie == false then return end
            _abAcc = _abAcc + dt
            if _abAcc < 0.1 then return end
            _abAcc = 0
            antiBoogieOnce()
        end)
    end

    local _acc = 0
    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        _acc = _acc + dt
        if _acc < 0.12 then return end 
        _acc = 0

        if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then
            snapshotCarry()
            wasStealing = true
        elseif wasStealing then
            wasStealing = false
            local who, tool = nearestThreat(55)
            if tool and (tostring(tool):lower():find("body swap") or tostring(tool):lower():find("boogie")
                or tostring(tool):lower():find("slap") or tostring(tool):lower():find("ragdoll")
                or tostring(tool):lower():find("medusa") or tostring(tool):lower():find("gummy")) then
                reportGrief(tool, who, tool)
                goBackToPet()
            elseif who and (os.clock() - (carry.t or 0)) < 4 then
                reportGrief("Steal interrupted", who, tool)
                goBackToPet()
            end
        end

        do
            local holder, hDist = nearestBoogieHolder(50)
            local realFx = hasBoogieEffect(LP.Character)
            local ragNow = isRagdolled()
            if holder and (realFx or (ragNow and not wasRag)) then
                local now = os.clock()
                if now - _abLast > 4 then
                    _abLast = now
                    reportGrief("Boogie Bomb", holder, "Boogie Bomb")
                    task.defer(antiBoogieOnce)
                    if carry.active or LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then
                        goBackToPet()
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local rag = isRagdolled()
        if rag and not wasRag then
            local who, tool = nearestThreat(55)
            reportGrief(tool or "Ragdoll / Slap", who, tool)
        end
        if wasRag and not rag then
            goBackToPet()
        end
        wasRag = rag
    end)

    local function ensureTrackerGui()
        local pg = LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
        if not pg then return nil end
        local sg = pg:FindFirstChild("JewskiStealTracker")
        if sg then return sg end
        sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        sg.Name = "JewskiStealTracker"
        sg.ResetOnSpawn = false
        sg.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        sg.DisplayOrder = 80
        sg.Enabled = false
        sg.Parent = pg
        local root = Instance.new("Frame")
        root.Name = "Root"
        root.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(300, 220)
        local sx = tonumber(_G._stp_stealTrackerX) or 16
        local sy = tonumber(_G._stp_stealTrackerY) or 180
        root.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(sx, sy)
        root.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(14, 14, 20)
        root.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
        root.BorderSizePixel = 0
        root.Active = true
        root.Parent = sg
        Instance.new("UICorner", root).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
        local title = Instance.new("TextButton", root)
        title.Name = "TitleDrag"
        title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 26)
        title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4)
        title.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 32)
        title.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        title.BorderSizePixel = 0
        title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        title.TextSize = 13
        title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        title.Text = "  STEAL TRACKER  ·  drag me"
        title.AutoButtonColor = false
        Instance.new("UICorner", title).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        do
            local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
            local dragging, dragStart, startPos = false, nil, nil
            title.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
                if _G.JewskiLockUi or (Config and Config.UILocked) then return end
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                    or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    dragging = true
                    dragStart = input.Position
                    startPos = root.Position
                    input.Changed:Connect(function()
                        if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                            dragging = false
                            local abs = root.AbsolutePosition
                            _G._stp_stealTrackerX = math.floor(abs.X)
                            _G._stp_stealTrackerY = math.floor(abs.Y)
                            if type(_G.JewskiSaveConfigNow) == "function" then
                                pcall(_G.JewskiSaveConfigNow)
                            elseif type(SaveConfig) == "function" then
                                pcall(SaveConfig)
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end)
            UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
                if not dragging then return end
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
                    or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    local d = input.Position - dragStart
                    root.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(
                        startPos.X.Offset + d.X,
                        startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y
                    )
                end
            end)
        end
        local list = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame", root)
        list.Name = "List"
        list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -40)
        list.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 34)
        list.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        list.BorderSizePixel = 0
        list.ScrollBarThickness = 3
        list.CanvasSize = UDim2.new()
        list.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        Instance.new("UIListLayout", list).Padding = UDim.new(0, 3)
        return sg
    end

    local function resolveStealBrainrot(pl)
        local name, gen = nil, 0
        local hrp = pl.Character and pl.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local animals = _G.JewskiStealPetList or _G.SharedState and _G.SharedState.AllAnimalsCache or {}
        if hrp and type(animals) == "table" then
            local bestD = 45
            for _, a in ipairs(animals) do
                local pos = a.position or a.Position or a.pos
                local g = tonumber(a.mps or a.mpsValue or a.genValue or a.gen) or 0
                local nm = a.name or a.Name or a.animal
                if typeof(pos) == "Vector3" then
                    local d = (pos - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                    if d < bestD then
                        bestD = d
                        if nm and tostring(nm) ~= "" then name = tostring(nm) end
                        gen = g
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if (not name or name == "?") and pl.Character then
            pcall(function()
                for _, m in ipairs(pl.Character:GetChildren()) do
                    if m:IsA("Model") or m:IsA("MeshPart") or m:IsA("BasePart") then
                        local n = tostring(m.Name or "")
                        if #n > 2 and not n:find("Humanoid") and n ~= "Handle" then
                            local low = n:lower()
                            if not low:find("part") and not low:find("attachment") then
                                name = n
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
        pcall(function()
            local an = pl:GetAttribute("StealingAnimal") or pl:GetAttribute("AnimalName")
            if type(an) == "string" and #an > 0 then name = an end
            local g = pl:GetAttribute("StealingGen") or pl:GetAttribute("Gen")
            if tonumber(g) then gen = tonumber(g) end
        end)
        return name or "Brainrot", gen
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(1.2)
            if _G.JewskiStealTracker ~= true then
                local pg = LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
                local sg = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("JewskiStealTracker")
                if sg then sg.Enabled = false end
            else
                pcall(function()
                    local sg = ensureTrackerGui()
                    if not sg then return end
                    sg.Enabled = true
                    local list = sg:FindFirstChild("Root") and sg.Root:FindFirstChild("List")
                    if not list then return end
                    for _, ch in ipairs(list:GetChildren()) do
                        if ch:IsA("TextLabel") then ch:Destroy() end
                    end
                    local minG = tonumber(_G.JewskiStealTrackerMinGen) or 0
                    local any = false
                    for _, pl in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                        if pl ~= LP and pl:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then
                            local nm, gen = resolveStealBrainrot(pl)
                            if gen >= minG or minG <= 0 then
                                any = true
                                local row = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                                row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 28)
                                row.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(24, 24, 32)
                                row.BackgroundTransparency = 0
                                row.BorderSizePixel = 0
                                row.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
                                row.TextSize = 11
                                row.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 240)
                                row.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                                row.Text = string.format("  %s  →  %s  (%s/s)", pl.Name, nm, shortGen(gen))
                                row.Parent = list
                                Instance.new("UICorner", row).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if not any then
                        local row = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                        row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 26)
                        row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        row.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
                        row.TextSize = 11
                        row.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(140, 140, 160)
                        row.Text = "  No active steals..."
                        row.Parent = list
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end)
end

do

local h = nil
pcall(function() if gethui then h = gethui() end end)
if not h then
h = LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 5)
end
return h
end
local function getList()
if type(_G.SHARED_PRIORITY_ITEMS) ~= "table" then _G.SHARED_PRIORITY_ITEMS = {} end
return _G.SHARED_PRIORITY_ITEMS
end
local function persist()
pcall(function()
if type(SaveConfig) == "function" then SaveConfig() end
if type(_G.JewskiSaveConfigNow) == "function" then _G.JewskiSaveConfigNow() end
end)
pcall(function()
if not writefile then return end
local HS = game:GetService("HttpService")
local list = getList()
local blob = {}
if readfile then
local ok, raw = pcall(readfile, "jewskivpriv.json")
if ok and type(raw) == "string" and #raw > 0 then
local ok2, d = pcall(HS.JSONDecode, HS, raw)
if ok2 and type(d) == "table" then blob = d end
end
end
local out = {}
for i, v in ipairs(list) do
if type(v) == "string" and v ~= "" then out[#out + 1] = v end
end
blob.priorityItems = out
writefile("jewskivpriv.json", HS:JSONEncode(blob))
end)
_G.JewskiPriVersion = (_G.JewskiPriVersion or 0) + 1
end

local d = i.Position - from

end)

local function makeRow(i, name)
local row = Instance.new("Frame")
row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 30)
row.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 30)
row.BackgroundTransparency = 0

num.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 30)
