num.BackgroundTransparency = 1
num.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
num.TextSize = 11
num.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(140, 160, 200)
num.Text = tostring(i) .. "."

local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -120, 1, 0)
lbl.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 0)
lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
lbl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham

local function mkB(txt, x, col, fn)
local b = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
b.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 22)
b.Position = UDim2.new(1, x, 0.5, -11)
b.BackgroundColor3 = col
b.Text = txt
b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
b.TextSize = 10
b.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
b.BorderSizePixel = 0
Instance.new("UICorner", b).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(fn)
end
mkB("^", -86, Color3.fromRGB(50, 100, 200), function()
local L = getList()
if i > 1 then L[i], L[i - 1] = L[i - 1], L[i]; persist(); refresh() end
end)
mkB("v", -56, Color3.fromRGB(50, 100, 200), function()
local L = getList()
if i < #L then L[i], L[i + 1] = L[i + 1], L[i]; persist(); refresh() end
end)
mkB("X", -28, Color3.fromRGB(160, 50, 50), function()
local L = getList()
table.remove(L, i)
persist()
refresh()
end)
end
refresh = function()
if not listFrame then return end
for _, ch in ipairs(listFrame:GetChildren()) do
if ch:IsA("Frame") then ch:Destroy() end
end
local L = getList()
for i, n in ipairs(L) do
makeRow(i, n)
end
end
local function addName()
local n = tostring(addBox.Text or ""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
if not n or n == "" then return end
local L = getList()
for _, v in ipairs(L) do
if tostring(v):lower() == n:lower() then addBox.Text = ""; return end
end
L[#L + 1] = n
addBox.Text = ""
persist()
refresh()
end
addBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(addName)
addBox.FocusLost:Connect(function(enter)
if enter then addName() end
end)
refresh()
return sg
end

pcall(function()
local ui = ensureUI()
if not ui then return end
ui.Enabled = not ui.Enabled
if ui.Enabled and type(refresh) == "function" then refresh() end
end)
end
_G.JewskiTogglePriorityMenu = _G.Jewski_TogglePriorityMenu

pcall(function()
if type(refresh) == "function" then refresh() end
end)

local function goToBrainrot(petPos, slot)
if type(_G.__VPRIV_goToBrainrot) == "function" then
return _G.__VPRIV_goToBrainrot(petPos, slot)
end
if type(_G.Jewski_GoToBrainrot) == "function" then
return _G.Jewski_GoToBrainrot(petPos, slot)
end
if type(_G.__VPRIV_goToBrainrot) == "function" then
return _G.__VPRIV_goToBrainrot(petPos, slot)
end
end
local function goToBrainrotPet(pet)
local resolved, pos = _resolveSwitchPet(pet)
if not resolved or not pos then return false end
_G.__jewskiChosenPet = resolved
task.spawn(function()
pcall(goToBrainrot, pos)
end)
return true
end
_G.JewskiGoToBrainrotPet = goToBrainrotPet
_G.JewskiTryGoOnTargetSwitch = function(pet)
if type(pet) ~= "table" then return false end
local plotName = pet.plot
local slot = pet.slot or pet.Slot
local pos = pet.position or pet.Position
if typeof(pos) ~= "Vector3" then
pcall(function()
local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
local plotInst = plots and plotName and plots:FindFirstChild(plotName)
if plotInst and type(getPetPosition) == "function" then
pos = getPetPosition(plotInst, slot)
elseif plotInst and type(_G.Jewski_GetPetPosition) == "function" then
pos = _G.Jewski_GetPetPosition(plotInst, slot)
end
end)
end
if typeof(pos) ~= "Vector3" then return false end
local inside = false
pcall(function()
local hrp = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not hrp then return end
local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
local plot = plots and plotName and plots:FindFirstChild(plotName)
if plot then
local ok, pp = pcall(function() return plot:GetPivot().Position end)
if ok and pp then
local rad = (pos.Y <= 9) and 32 or 28
inside = math.abs(hrp.Position.X - pp.X) < rad and math.abs(hrp.Position.Z - pp.Z) < rad
end
end
if not inside and type(getClosestBaseIdx) == "function" and type(BASES_LOW) == "table" then
local pIdx = getClosestBaseIdx(pos)
local mIdx = getClosestBaseIdx(hrp.Position)
if pIdx and pIdx == mIdx then
local b = BASES_LOW[mIdx]
if b and math.abs(hrp.Position.X - b.X) <= 62 and math.abs(hrp.Position.Z - b.Z) <= 58 then
inside = true
end
end
end
end)
if not inside then return false end
_G._goBrGen = (_G._goBrGen or 0) + 1
_goBrGen = _G._goBrGen
local gtb = _G.Jewski_GoToBrainrot or _G.__VPRIV_goToBrainrot or goToBrainrot
task.spawn(function()
pcall(gtb, pos, slot)
end)
return true
end
local _VIM_TP = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
local function fireGrappleBeforeTP(destino)
if type(_G.JewskiFireGrapple2) ~= "function" then
_G.Jewski_Step("TP: grapple avant TP", "JewskiFireGrapple2 indisponible")
return false
end
local g = _waitGrapple(5)
local char = LP.Character
local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not char or not hum then
_G.Jewski_Step("TP: grapple avant TP", "pas de perso")
return false
end
local justEquipped = false
if not char:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook") then
g = g or findTool("Grapple Hook")
if g then
pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(g) end)
justEquipped = true
end
end
if justEquipped or not char:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook") then
pcall(function()
local waitS = math.max(0.05, (tonumber(_G.jewskiGrappleWaitMs) or 200) / 1000)
char:WaitForChild("Grapple Hook", waitS)
end)
local c = LP.Character
if c and c ~= char then
char = c
hum = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
local g2 = findTool("Grapple Hook")
if g2 and hum and not char:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook") then
pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(g2) end)
justEquipped = true
pcall(function() char:WaitForChild("Grapple Hook", 0.4) end)
end
end
end
if justEquipped then
task.wait(math.min(0.03, (tonumber(_G.jewskiGrappleSettleMs) or 30) / 1000))
end
local c = LP.Character
if not (c and c:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook")) then
_G.Jewski_Step("TP: grapple avant TP", "hook pas en main")
return false
end
local ok, res = pcall(_G.JewskiFireGrapple2, destino)
_G.__jewskiGrapplePrimed = true
_G.Jewski_Step("TP: grapple avant TP", (ok and res) and "OK" or ("echec " .. tostring(res)))
RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
return ok and res and true or false
end
local function _avatarScale(char)
local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not hum then return 1 end
local scale = 1
pcall(function()
scale = math.max(scale,
tonumber(hum.BodyHeightScale) or 1,
tonumber(hum.BodyDepthScale) or 1,
tonumber(hum.BodyWidthScale) or 1,
(tonumber(hum.HipHeight) or 2) / 2)
end)
return math.clamp(scale, 1, 3)
end
local function _cloneStandPos(hrp, destPos, facingDir)
    if not (hrp and facingDir and facingDir.Magnitude > 0.1) then
        return destPos
    end
    local axis = facingDir.Unit
    local toPlayer = hrp.Position - destPos
    local sign = (axis:Dot(toPlayer) >= 0) and 1 or -1
    local backoff = tonumber(_G.JewskiCloneBackoff) or tonumber(_G.jewskiCloneBackoff) or 0.5
    return destPos + axis * sign * backoff
end
local function _cloneStandCF(hrp, destPos, facingDir)
    local stand = _cloneStandPos(hrp, destPos, facingDir)
    local face = (facingDir and facingDir.Magnitude > 0.1) and facingDir.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
    return CFrame.new(stand, stand + face)
end
local function doVelocityTP(forceGrapple, _preScanned)
local t0 = os.clock()
while os.clock() - t0 < 8 do
if type(_G.__VPRIV_doVelocityTP) == "function" and _G.__VPRIV_TP_READY == true then
return _G.__VPRIV_doVelocityTP(forceGrapple, _preScanned)
end
if type(_G.JewskiStartSideTP) == "function" and _G.__VPRIV_TP_READY == true then
return _G.JewskiStartSideTP()
end
task.wait(0.05)
end
warn("[JEWSKI] TP engine not ready")
end
_G.JewskiStartSideTP = doVelocityTP
local function _petIsPriority(p)
if not p then return false end
return (_priIndexOf(p.index) or _priIndexOf(p.name)) ~= nil
end
local function _petMeetsTPGen(p)
if not p then return false end
if _petIsPriority(p) then return true end
if not _G.__JEWSKI_TpOnLoadFilterActive then
return true
end
local minG = tonumber(_G.JewskiMinGen) or 20000000
if minG <= 0 then minG = 20000000 end
local m = tonumber(p.mps) or 0
if m <= 0 then m = tonumber(p.genValue) or 0 end
return m >= minG
end
local function pickTPWinner(pets)
if type(pets) ~= "table" then return nil, false end
local man = _G.JewskiManualStealUID
if type(man) == "string" and man ~= "" then
local locked = _findPetByUid(pets, man)
if locked and _petAllowed(locked) and _petMeetsTPGen(locked) then
return locked, _petIsPriority(locked)
end
end
local mode = tostring(_G.JewskiStealMode or "priority"):lower()
local list = _buildStealPetList(pets, mode)
local filtered = {}
for _, p in ipairs(list) do
if _petAllowed(p) and _petMeetsTPGen(p) then
filtered[#filtered + 1] = p
end
end
if #filtered == 0 then return nil, false end
local shown = _G.JewskiStealPetList
if type(shown) == "table" and shown[1] then
local uid = _petUid(shown[1])
local live = uid and (_findPetByUid(filtered, uid) or _findPetByUid(pets, uid))
if live and _petAllowed(live) and _petMeetsTPGen(live) then
return live, _petIsPriority(live)
end
end
local winner = filtered[1]
if not winner then return nil, false end
return winner, _petIsPriority(winner)
end
local function launchSideTP(pets, winner)
if isTeleporting then return false end
if type(pets) ~= "table" or #pets == 0 then return false end
local c = LP.Character
if not (c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")) then return false end
_G.__jewskiRadarOff = true
_G.jewskiFreezeUntil = os.clock() + 10
_G.__jewskiChosenPet = winner
if winner then
_G.Jewski_Step("cible choisie", tostring(winner.name) .. "  " .. tostring(winner.mps) .. "/s")
end
_lastTPOk = false
local ok, res = pcall(doVelocityTP, false, pets)
return ok and res == true
end
local function runSideTPLaunch(maxWait, checkAuto)
local _t0 = os.clock()
local _gotOnce = false
local _tpDelay = tonumber(_G._stp_tpDelay) or tonumber(_G.TPDelay) or 0
local _delayed = false
local _readyT = nil
local _lastUid = nil
maxWait = tonumber(maxWait) or 45
while os.clock() - _t0 < maxWait do
if checkAuto and _G.jewskiAutoTP == false then return end
if not isTeleporting then
local ok, pets = pcall(scanAllPets, true)
if ok and pets and #pets > 0 then
if not _gotOnce then _G.Jewski_Step("1er pet detecte", "#" .. tostring(#pets)) end
_gotOnce = true
local hasPriority = false
local _winner = nil
_winner, hasPriority = pickTPWinner(pets)
local uid = _winner and (_petUid(_winner) or tostring(_winner.uid or ""))
if not (uid and uid ~= "" and uid == _lastUid) then
_lastUid = uid
_readyT = nil
end
local _c = LP.Character
local _carpetOk = false
if _c then
for _, n in ipairs(CARPET_NAMES) do
local t = _c:FindFirstChild(n)
if t and t:IsA("Tool") then
_carpetOk = true
if not _G.__seqCarpet then _G.__seqCarpet = true; _G.Jewski_Step("carpet EN MAIN") end
break
end
end
end
local _prioNow = hasPriority
if _winner and _readyT == nil then
_readyT = os.clock()
end
local _carpetWait = (tonumber(_G.jewskiCarpetWaitMs) or 200) / 1000
if not _carpetOk then pcall(equipCarpet) end
local _carpetGate = _carpetOk
or (_readyT ~= nil and (os.clock() - _readyT) >= _carpetWait)
local _engageBlock = _carpetEngaging and (os.clock() - _t0) < 6
if (not _engageBlock) and _c and _c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and _carpetGate then
if _winner then
if false and _tpDelay > 0 and not _delayed and not _prioNow then
_delayed = true; task.wait(_tpDelay)
local ok2, pets2 = pcall(scanAllPets, true)
if ok2 and type(pets2) == "table" then
local w2 = pickTPWinner(pets2)
if _petUid(w2) ~= uid then
_lastUid = _petUid(w2)
_readyT = nil
else
pets, _winner = pets2, w2 or _winner
end
end
end
if _winner and _petUid(_winner) == uid then
if launchSideTP(pets, _winner) then
return
end
end
task.wait(0.35)
end
end
end
end
RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
end
end

local function _normalizeStealPet(pet)
if type(pet) ~= "table" then
return nil
end
if pet.plot ~= nil then
pet.plot = tostring(pet.plot)
end
if pet.slot ~= nil then
pet.slot = tostring(pet.slot)
end
if pet.plot and pet.slot then
pet.uid = tostring(pet.plot) .. "_" .. tostring(pet.slot)
end
return pet
end
local _intendedStealPet = function(pets)
pets = pets or _G.JewskiStealPetList
if type(pets) ~= "table" then
pets = {}
end
local man = _G.JewskiManualStealUID
if type(man) == "string" and man ~= "" then
local pet = _findPetByUid(pets, man)
if pet and _petAllowed(pet) then
return _normalizeStealPet(pet)
end
return nil
end
local mode = tostring(_G.JewskiStealMode or "priority"):lower()
if mode == "highest" then
mode = "value"
end
local picked = _pickPetByMode(pets, mode)
if picked and _petAllowed(picked) then
return _normalizeStealPet(picked)
end
return nil
end
local function _applyStealTarget(pet)
pet = _normalizeStealPet(pet)
if not pet then
return nil
end
if pet.plot and pet.slot then
pet.uid = tostring(pet.plot) .. "_" .. tostring(pet.slot)
elseif not pet.uid then
pet.uid = _petUid(pet)
end
_G.JewskiStealTarget = pet
_G.JewskiSelectedPetData = pet
_G.JewskiManualStealUID = pet.uid
_G.JewskiStealTargetUID = pet.uid
P.SelectedPetData = pet
if type(SharedState) == "table" then
SharedState.SelectedPetData = pet
end
_G.__jewskiChosenPet = pet
return pet
end
_G.JewskiApplyStealTarget = _applyStealTarget
local function _forceSetPriorityTarget(pets)
local manUid = _G.JewskiManualStealUID
if type(manUid) == "string" and manUid ~= "" and type(pets) == "table" then
for _, pet in ipairs(pets) do
local uid = pet.uid or (type(_petUid) == "function" and _petUid(pet))
if tostring(uid) == tostring(manUid) then
return _applyStealTarget(pet)
end
end
end
local best = _intendedStealPet(pets)
if not best then
_priDbg("noPriPet", "No steal-list pet found")
return nil
end
best = _applyStealTarget(best)
if best and _G.__JewskiPriTargetUid ~= best.uid then
_G.__JewskiPriTargetUid = best.uid
end
return best
end
local function _selectPriorityTarget(pets)
return _forceSetPriorityTarget(pets)
end
local function _readLoadedStealList()
_ensureSyncMod()
local list = _G.JewskiStealPetList
if type(list) == "table" and #list > 0 then
local pet = _intendedStealPet(list)
if _stealPetReady(pet) then
return list, pet
end
end
local ok, scanned = pcall(scanAllPets, true)
if not ok or type(scanned) ~= "table" then
return list, nil
end
list = _buildStealPetList(scanned, _G.JewskiStealMode)
_G.JewskiStealPetList = list
P.AllAnimalsCache = list
return list, _intendedStealPet(list)
end
local function _waitStealTargetReady(timeout)
timeout = tonumber(timeout) or 45
local t0 = os.clock()
local lastPet, lastPets
while os.clock() - t0 < timeout do
if _G.JewskiTPStop or _G.__JewskiCancelAutoTP then
return nil
end
local list, pet = _readLoadedStealList()
pcall(function()
if type(_G.JewskiOnStealList) == "function" then
_G.JewskiOnStealList(list or _G.JewskiStealPetList)
end
end)
if _stealPetReady(pet) then
return _applyStealTarget(pet), list
end
lastPet, lastPets = pet, list
RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
end
if _stealPetReady(lastPet) then
return _applyStealTarget(lastPet), lastPets
end
return nil, lastPets or _G.JewskiStealPetList
end
local _syncStealFlags = function()
local auto = _G.JewskiAutoSteal == true
local mode = tostring(_G.JewskiStealMode or "priority"):lower()
if mode == "highest" then
mode = "value"
end
_G.NEAREST_INSTANT_MODE = auto and mode == "nearest"
local pet = _intendedStealPet(_G.JewskiStealPetList)
if pet then
_applyStealTarget(pet)
end
if auto then
if pet then
P.SelectedPetData = pet
elseif type(_G.JewskiManualStealUID) ~= "string" or _G.JewskiManualStealUID == "" then
P.SelectedPetData = nil
end
else
P.SelectedPetData = nil
_G.NEAREST_INSTANT_MODE = false
end
end
_G.JewskiSyncNearestInstant = _syncStealFlags

end

local SAFE_POLL_RATE = 0.05
local SAFE_POLL_OVERRIDE_UNTIL = 0
function _G.getSafePollRate()

function _G.triggerSafePollBoost()
SAFE_POLL_OVERRIDE_UNTIL = os.clock() + 3
end
local FIRE_DEBOUNCE = 0.12
local FIRE_BURST = 4
local ENABLE_BURST = 35
local ENABLE_DEBOUNCE = 0.00
local ENABLE_COOLDOWN = 0.08
local function getHRP()
local char = LP.Character
return char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end
local function getBoxIndex(pos)
for i, b in ipairs(boxes) do
if pos.X >= math.min(b.min.X, b.max.X) and pos.X <= math.max(b.min.X, b.max.X)
and pos.Z >= math.min(b.min.Z, b.max.Z) and pos.Z <= math.max(b.min.Z, b.max.Z) then
return i
end
end
end
local function getPromptPosition(prompt)
local p = prompt.Parent
if not p then return end
if p:IsA("Attachment") and p.Parent then p = p.Parent end
if p:IsA("BasePart") then return p.Position elseif p:IsA("Model") then return p:GetPivot().Position end
end
local promptMatchesSelectedPet = function(prompt)
local promptUid = _promptPetUid(prompt)
local man = _G.JewskiManualStealUID
if type(man) == "string" and man ~= "" then
return promptUid ~= nil and tostring(promptUid) == tostring(man)
end
local selected = (P and P.SelectedPetData) or _G.JewskiStealTarget or _G.JewskiSelectedPetData
if type(selected) ~= "table" then return false end
local targetUid = selected.uid or _petUid(selected)
if selected.plot and selected.slot then
targetUid = tostring(selected.plot) .. "_" .. tostring(selected.slot)
end
if promptUid and targetUid and tostring(promptUid) == tostring(targetUid) then
return true
end
return false
end
local isPromptAvailable = function(prompt, hrpPos)
if _G.JewskiAutoSteal ~= true then return false end
if not prompt or not prompt.Parent or not prompt.Enabled then return false end
local pos = getPromptPosition(prompt)
if not pos then return false end
local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
if plots then
local parentPlot = prompt:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("Model")
while parentPlot and parentPlot.Parent ~= plots do parentPlot = parentPlot.Parent end
if parentPlot then
local sign = parentPlot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
if sign then
local gui = sign:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SurfaceGui", true)
local label = gui and gui:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel", true)
if label then
local txt = label.Text:lower()
if txt:find(LP.Name:lower(), 1, true) or txt:find(LP.DisplayName:lower(), 1, true) then
return false
end
end
end
end
end
local manLocked = type(_G.JewskiManualStealUID) == "string" and _G.JewskiManualStealUID ~= ""
if manLocked or _G.NEAREST_INSTANT_MODE ~= true then
if not promptMatchesSelectedPet(prompt) then return false end
end
return (pos - hrpPos).Magnitude <= (CONFIG.RADIUS or 75)
end
local function canFire(prompt, debounce)
local t = os.clock()
local last = lastFire[prompt]
if last and (t - last) < debounce then return false end
lastFire[prompt] = t
return true
end
local function firePrompt(prompt, burst, debounce)
if not prompt or not prompt.Parent or not prompt.Enabled or not canFire(prompt, debounce) then return end
local fireFn = fireproximityprompt or _G.__JewskiFirePrompt
if type(fireFn) ~= "function" then return end
do
local dur = tonumber(prompt.HoldDuration) or tonumber(_G.JewskiStealHoldDuration) or 1.3
if not dur or dur <= 0 then dur = 1.3 end
local st = _G.Jewski_StealStatus
if type(st) ~= "table" then st = {}; _G.Jewski_StealStatus = st end
st.target = st.target or _G.JewskiStealTarget
st.duration = dur
local now = tick()
if st.active and st.start then
local p = (now - st.start) / dur
if p >= 1 then
st.start = now
st.active = true
end
else
st.active = true
st.start = now
end
end
for _ = 1, burst do
pcall(function() fireFn(prompt, 0) end)
end
end
local function trackPrompt(prompt)
if trackedPrompts[prompt] then return end
trackedPrompts[prompt] = true
local function tryInstantEnableFire()
if _G.JewskiAutoSteal ~= true then return end
local hrp = getHRP()
if hrp and isPromptAvailable(prompt, hrp.Position) then
CONFIG.AUTO_STEAL = true
local now = os.clock()
local le = lastEnableFire[prompt]
if not le or (now - le) >= ENABLE_COOLDOWN then
lastEnableFire[prompt] = now
firePrompt(prompt, ENABLE_BURST, ENABLE_DEBOUNCE)
end
end
end
task.defer(tryInstantEnableFire)
pcall(function()
prompt:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"):Connect(function()
if prompt.Enabled then tryInstantEnableFire() end
end)
end)
prompt.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
if not prompt:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
trackedPrompts[prompt] = nil
lastFire[prompt] = nil
lastEnableFire[prompt] = nil
end
end)
end
local function scanBrainrotPrompts()
local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
if not plots then return end
for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
local podiums = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
if podiums then
for _, podium in ipairs(podiums:GetChildren()) do
local prompt = select(1, _stealPromptForSlot(plot, podium.Name))
if prompt then trackPrompt(prompt) end
end
end
end
end
_G.JewskiAfterTpLoad(function()
pcall(scanBrainrotPrompts)
workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
if obj.ClassName ~= "ProximityPrompt" then return end
if obj:FindFirstAncestor("AnimalPodiums") then
trackPrompt(obj)
end
end)
task.spawn(function()
while task.wait(12) do
if not _G.__JEWSKI_LIGHT_MODE then pcall(scanBrainrotPrompts) end
end
end)
task.spawn(function()
while task.wait(_G.getSafePollRate()) do
_G.NEAREST_INSTANT_MODE = (_G.JewskiAutoSteal == true and tostring(_G.JewskiStealMode or ""):lower() == "nearest")
if _G.JewskiAutoSteal == true then
local hrp = getHRP()
if not hrp then
CONFIG.AUTO_STEAL = false
else
local myPos = hrp.Position
local anyAvailable = false
for prompt in pairs(trackedPrompts) do
if isPromptAvailable(prompt, myPos) then
anyAvailable = true
if CONFIG.AUTO_STEAL then
firePrompt(prompt, FIRE_BURST, FIRE_DEBOUNCE)
end
end
end
CONFIG.AUTO_STEAL = anyAvailable
end
else
CONFIG.AUTO_STEAL = false
end
end
end)
end)
_G.JewskiScanBrainrotPrompts = scanBrainrotPrompts
_G.JewskiGetBoxIndex = getBoxIndex
end

pcall(function()
local existing = guiParent:FindFirstChild("AutoStealCurrentTargetHUD")
if existing then existing:Destroy() end
local pg = LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
local oldPg = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("AutoStealCurrentTargetHUD")
if oldPg then oldPg:Destroy() end
if gethui then
local h = gethui()
local oldH = h and h:FindFirstChild("AutoStealCurrentTargetHUD")
if oldH and oldH ~= existing then oldH:Destroy() end
end
end)
