	local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not char or not hum then
		_G.Jewski_Step("TP: grapple avant TP", "pas de perso")
		return false
	end
	local justEquipped = false
	if not char:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook") then
		g = g or findTool("Grapple Hook")
		if g then
			hum:EquipTool(g)
			justEquipped = true
		end
	end
	if justEquipped or not char:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook") then
		local waitS = math.max(0.05, (tonumber(_G.jewskiGrappleWaitMs) or 200) / 1000)
			char:WaitForChild("Grapple Hook", waitS)
		local c = LP.Character
		if c and c ~= char then
			char = c
			hum = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			local g2 = findTool("Grapple Hook")
			if g2 and hum and not char:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook") then
				hum:EquipTool(g2) 
				justEquipped = true
				char:WaitForChild("Grapple Hook", 0.4) 
			end
		end
	end
	if justEquipped then
		task.wait((tonumber(_G.jewskiGrappleSettleMs) or 60) / 1000)
	end
	local c = LP.Character
	if not (c and c:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook")) then
		_G.Jewski_Step("TP: grapple avant TP", "hook pas en main")
		return false
	end
	local res = _G.JewskiFireGrapple2(destino)
	_G.__jewskiGrapplePrimed = true
	_G.Jewski_Step("TP: grapple avant TP", (res) and "OK" or ("echec " .. tostring(res)))
	RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
	RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
	return res and true or false
end

local function doVelocityTP(forceGrapple, _preScanned)
	_G.__TP_T0 = os.clock()
	_G.Jewski_Step("TP: depart", "pets connus=" .. tostring(_preScanned and #_preScanned or "?"))

	if not _beginTpSession() then return false end
	local _tpOk, _tpErr = pcall(function()
	_cloneTP = false
	_cloneFired = false
	_G.JewskiCloneTP = false
	_G.JewskiCloneFired = false
	_G.jewskiTPStop = false
	if type(_G.JewskiSyncBabySharkTpGlobals) == "function" then _G.JewskiSyncBabySharkTpGlobals() end

	local function _abortTp()
		_G.jewskiStealHold = false
		endTP()
	end

	local char = LP.Character
	local hrp = char and (char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char.PrimaryPart)
	local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if char and (not hrp or not hum) then
		RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
		char = LP.Character
		hrp = char and (char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char.PrimaryPart)
		hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	end
	if not char or not hrp or not hum then _abortTp(); return end

	if _inVoid(hrp) or hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y < -40 then
		_waitOutOfVoid(12)
		if _G.jewskiTPStop then return _abortTp() end
		char = LP.Character
		hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if not hrp or not hum then return _abortTp() end
	end

	local allPets = _preScanned
	if type(allPets) ~= "table" or #allPets == 0 then
		allPets = scanForTP()
	end
	if type(allPets) ~= "table" or #allPets == 0 then return _abortTp() end

	local function _sameChosen(a, b)
		if not a or not b then return false end
		local ua, ub = _petUid(a), _petUid(b)
		if ua and ub then return ua == ub end
		return tostring(a.plot) == tostring(b.plot) and tostring(a.slot) == tostring(b.slot)
	end
	local function _refreshLocked(lock, list)
		if not lock then return nil end
		if type(list) == "table" then
			for _, p in ipairs(list) do
				if _sameChosen(p, lock) then return p end
			end
		end
		return lock
	end

	local pet
	local _pre = _G.__jewskiChosenPet
	if _pre and _petAllowed(_pre) then
		local allowPre = true
		if _G.__JEWSKI_TpOnLoadFilterActive then
			local pri = false
			if type(_petIsPriority) == "function" then pri = _petIsPriority(_pre) end
			if not pri then
				local minG = tonumber(_G.JewskiMinGen) or 20000000
				if minG <= 0 then minG = 20000000 end
				local m = tonumber(_pre.mps) or tonumber(_pre.genValue) or 0
				if m < minG then allowPre = false end
			end
		end
		if allowPre then
			pet = _refreshLocked(_pre, allPets)
		end
	else
		pet = _resolveTPPet(allPets)
		if pet and _G.__JEWSKI_TpOnLoadFilterActive then
			local pri = false
			if type(_petIsPriority) == "function" then pri = _petIsPriority(pet) end
			if not pri then
				local minG = tonumber(_G.JewskiMinGen) or 20000000
				if minG <= 0 then minG = 20000000 end
				local m = tonumber(pet.mps) or tonumber(pet.genValue) or 0
				if m < minG then pet = nil end
			end
		end
	end
	if not pet then
		for _, p in ipairs(allPets) do
			if _petAllowed(p) then
				local allow = true
				if _G.__JEWSKI_TpOnLoadFilterActive then
					local pri = false
					if type(_petIsPriority) == "function" then pri = _petIsPriority(p) end
					if not pri then
						local minG = tonumber(_G.JewskiMinGen) or 20000000
						if minG <= 0 then minG = 20000000 end
						local m = tonumber(p.mps) or tonumber(p.genValue) or 0
						if m < minG then allow = false end
					end
				end
				if allow then pet = p; break end
			end
		end
	end
	if pet and _G.__JEWSKI_TpOnLoadFilterActive then
		local pri = false
		if type(_petIsPriority) == "function" then pri = _petIsPriority(pet) end
		if not pri then
			local minG = tonumber(_G.JewskiMinGen) or 20000000
			if minG <= 0 then minG = 20000000 end
			local m = tonumber(pet.mps) or tonumber(pet.genValue) or 0
			if m < minG then pet = nil end
		end
	end
	if pet then
		_G.__jewskiChosenPet = pet
	end

	if not pet or not pet.position then return _abortTp() end

	local petPos = pet.position
	local petName = pet.name

	local grappled = fireGrappleBeforeTP(petPos)

	_G.jewskiStealHold = true
	task.delay(15, function() _G.jewskiStealHold = false end)

	local petY = petPos.Y
	if TALL_PETS[petName] then petY = petPos.Y - TALL_OFFSET end

	local coordTable = (petY > 23.15) and UPPER or LOWER

	local closestData, skyKey = findClosest(petPos, coordTable)
	if not closestData or not skyKey then return _abortTp() end

	local destPos = closestData.coord

	local _carpet
	do
		local _c = LP.Character
		local _have = false
		if _c then
			for _, n in ipairs(CARPET_NAMES) do
				local t = _c:FindFirstChild(n)
				if t and t:IsA("Tool") then _have = true; _carpet = n; break end
			end
		end
		if grappled then
			if not _have then
				task.wait(0.06)
				task.wait(0.06)
				local _tc = os.clock()
				repeat
					_carpet = equipCarpet()
					local c = LP.Character
					if _carpet and c and c:FindFirstChild(_carpet) then break end
					RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
				until os.clock() - _tc > 0.4
			end
		else
			grappled = fireGrappleBeforeTP(petPos)
			task.wait(0.06)
			task.wait(0.06)
			local _tc = os.clock()
			repeat
				_carpet = equipCarpet()
				local c = LP.Character
				if _carpet and c and c:FindFirstChild(_carpet) then break end
				RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			until os.clock() - _tc > 0.4
		end
	end
	vZero(hrp)

	local facingDir = closestData.facing == "NORTH" and Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
	local function keepTPFacing(dirVec)
		if not hrp or not hrp.Parent or not dirVec then return end
		local d = dirVec.Unit
		if d.Magnitude < 0.01 then return end
		hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + d)
		hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
		hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
	end

	local _frontApproach = false
	do
		local isUpper = (coordTable == UPPER)
		local idx = getClosestBaseIdx(petPos)
		local frontCoord, frontFace = buildFrontCandidate(idx, isUpper, hrp.Position.Z)
		local bestCoord, bestFace = frontCoord, frontFace
		local bestDist = (hrp.Position - frontCoord).Magnitude
		local pickedFront = true

		local _tb = BASES_LOW[idx]
		local _isWest = idx <= 4
		local _rowBlocked = false

		local _dx, _dz = hrp.Position.X - _tb.X, hrp.Position.Z - _tb.Z
		local _distToBase = math.sqrt(_dx * _dx + _dz * _dz)
		local _sideRange = tonumber(_G.jewskiSideTPRange) or 100
		if _G.jewskiPreferFrontOnRow ~= false and _distToBase > _sideRange then
			for i = 1, 8 do
				if i ~= idx and (i <= 4) == _isWest then
					local bz = BASES_LOW[i].Z
					if (bz - hrp.Position.Z) * (bz - _tb.Z) < 0 then _rowBlocked = true; break end
				end
			end
		end

		if not _rowBlocked then
			for _, d in ipairs(plotSides(coordTable, idx)) do
				local dd = (hrp.Position - d.coord).Magnitude
				if dd < bestDist then
					bestDist = dd
					bestCoord = d.coord
					bestFace = d.facing == "NORTH" and Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
					pickedFront = false
				end
			end
		end
		destPos = bestCoord
		facingDir = bestFace
		_frontApproach = pickedFront
	end

	if facingDir and facingDir.Magnitude > 0.1 then
		local axis = facingDir.Unit
		local toPlayer = hrp.Position - destPos
		local sign = (axis:Dot(toPlayer) >= 0) and 1 or -1
		local backoff = tonumber(_G.JewskiCloneBackoff) or tonumber(_G.jewskiCloneBackoff) or tonumber(_G.JewskiCloneBackoff) or 0.5
		destPos = destPos + axis * sign * backoff
	end

	local _route = computeRoute(hrp.Position, destPos, facingDir, nil, false) 
	_G.Jewski_Step("route calculee", "waypoints=" .. tostring(_route and #_route or 0))

	local ASCEND_STEP = 10
	local _stepped = {}
	do
		local prev = hrp.Position
		for _, wp in ipairs(_route) do
			local dy = wp.Y - prev.Y
			if dy > ASCEND_STEP * 1.5 then
				local n = math.ceil(dy / ASCEND_STEP)
				for s = 1, n - 1 do
					local t = s / n
					_stepped[#_stepped + 1] = Vector3.new(
						prev.X + (wp.X - prev.X) * t,
						prev.Y + dy * t,
						prev.Z + (wp.Z - prev.Z) * t
					)
				end
			end
			_stepped[#_stepped + 1] = wp
			prev = wp
		end
	end
	local _mainSpeed = math.clamp(tonumber(_G.TPVelocity) or 400, 200, 750)
	_mainSpeed = _pingAdjustSpeed(_mainSpeed)
	do
		local closeSpd = tonumber(_G.JewskiCloseSpeed) or 80
		local closeDist = tonumber(_G.JewskiCloseDist) or 90
		local hrpNow = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if hrpNow and destPos then
			local flat = Vector3.new(hrpNow.Position.X - destPos.X, 0, hrpNow.Position.Z - destPos.Z).Magnitude
			if flat <= closeDist then
				_mainSpeed = math.clamp(closeSpd, 20, 500)
			end
		end
	end

	_G.Jewski_Step("VELOCITY: demarre", "vitesse=" .. tostring(math.floor(_mainSpeed)))
	local _hum = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if _hum then pcall(function() _hum.AutoRotate = false end) end
	local _faceConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then
			if _faceConn then _faceConn:Disconnect() end
			return
		end
		hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + facingDir)
		hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
	end)
	velMoveThrough(hrp, _stepped, _mainSpeed, true, true)
	if _faceConn then _faceConn:Disconnect() end
	if _hum then pcall(function() _hum.AutoRotate = true end) end
	if _G.jewskiTPStop then
		if hrp and hrp.Parent then vZero(hrp) end
		return _abortTp()
	end

	do
		local _runCap = _frontApproach and (tonumber(_G.jewskiFrontRunIn) or 130) or 400
		local _t0 = os.clock()
		while os.clock() - _t0 < 4 do
			if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then break end
			if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") or _G.jewskiTPStop then break end
			equipCarpet()
			local diff = destPos - hrp.Position
			local mag = diff.Magnitude
			if mag <= 3 then break end
			if diff.Y > 3 then
				local _hum = hrp.Parent and hrp.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				if _hum then
					local st = _hum:GetState()
					if st ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping and st ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall then
						_hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
						_hum.Jump = true
					end
				end
			end
			hrp.Velocity = diff.Unit * math.min(_runCap, mag * 8)
			hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			keepTPFacing(facingDir)
			RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
		end
	end

	if hrp and hrp.Parent and not _G.jewskiTPStop then
		local _flatOff = Vector3.new(destPos.X - hrp.Position.X, 0, destPos.Z - hrp.Position.Z).Magnitude
		if _flatOff > 10 then
			local _rr = (_stepped and #_stepped > 0) and _stepped or { destPos }
			velMoveThrough(hrp, _rr, _mainSpeed, true, true)
			if hrp and hrp.Parent then
				hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end
		end
	end

	if hrp and hrp.Parent then
		hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + (facingDir or Vector3.new(0,0,-1)))
	end
	vZero(hrp)

	local syncFrames = 2
	local syncConn
	syncConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then syncConn:Disconnect(); return end
		syncFrames = syncFrames - 1
		hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(destPos, destPos + (facingDir or Vector3.new(0,0,-1)))
		hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
		hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
		if syncFrames <= 0 then syncConn:Disconnect() end
	end)

	for _ = 1, 3 do
		RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
		local _hum = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or hum
		if _hum and _hum.Parent and _hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then break end
		if _G.jewskiTPStop then break end
	end

	do
		local _hrp = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if _hrp and _hrp.Parent then
			pcall(function()
				_hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(destPos, destPos + (facingDir or Vector3.new(0,0,-1)))
				_hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				_hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end)
		end
		for _ = 1, 2 do RunService.Heartbeat:Wait() end
	end

	if healConn then healConn:Disconnect() end
	_cloneTP = true
	_cloneFired = true
	_G.JewskiCloneTP = true
	_G.JewskiCloneFired = true
	_lastTPOk = true

	if _G.jewskiTPStop then _G.jewskiStealHold = false; endTP(); return end

	do
		local stable = 0
		for _ = 1, 18 do
			if _G.jewskiTPStop then break end
			local _hrp = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not _hrp or not _hrp.Parent then break end
			local flat = (Vector3.new(_hrp.Position.X, 0, _hrp.Position.Z) - Vector3.new(destPos.X, 0, destPos.Z)).Magnitude
			if flat <= 3.5 and math.abs(_hrp.Position.Y - destPos.Y) <= 4 then
				stable = stable + 1
				if stable >= 2 then break end
			else
				stable = 0
				_hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(destPos, destPos + facingDir) 
				_hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				_hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end
			RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
		end
	end
	if _G.jewskiTPStop then _G.jewskiStealHold = false; endTP(); return end

	do
		local _syncLeft = 5
		local _syncConn
		_syncConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then _syncConn:Disconnect(); return end
			_syncLeft = _syncLeft - 1
			pcall(function()
				hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(destPos, destPos + (facingDir or Vector3.new(0,0,-1)))
				hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end)
			if _syncLeft <= 0 then _syncConn:Disconnect() end
		end)
	end

	for _ = 1, 20 do
		task.wait(0.05)
		if hum and hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then break end
	end

	do
		local _stable, _st0 = 0, os.clock()
		while os.clock() - _st0 < 1.5 do
			if _G.jewskiTPStop then break end
			local _h = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not _h or not _h.Parent then break end
			local flat = (Vector3.new(_h.Position.X, 0, _h.Position.Z) - Vector3.new(destPos.X, 0, destPos.Z)).Magnitude
			if flat <= 3.5 and math.abs(_h.Position.Y - destPos.Y) <= 4 then
				_stable = _stable + 1
				if _stable >= 4 then break end
			else
				_stable = 0
				pcall(function()
					_h.CFrame = CFrame.new(destPos, destPos + (facingDir or Vector3.new(0,0,-1)))
					_h.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
					_h.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
				end)
			end
			RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
		end
	end

	local _ahrp = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local _clonePos = (_ahrp and _ahrp.Parent and _ahrp.Position) or destPos

	local _clonePlat = Instance.new("Part")
	_clonePlat.Name = "JewskiClonePlatform"
	_clonePlat.Size = Vector3.new(12, 1, 12)
	_clonePlat.Position = Vector3.new(_clonePos.X, _clonePos.Y - 3, _clonePos.Z)
	_clonePlat.Anchored = true
	_clonePlat.CanCollide = true
	_clonePlat.Transparency = 1
	_clonePlat.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
	_clonePlat.Parent = workspace

	if _ahrp and _ahrp.Parent then
		_ahrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
		_ahrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
	end

	local _preClonePos, _preCloneChar
	do
		_preCloneChar = LP.Character
		local _h = _preCloneChar and _preCloneChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		_preClonePos = _h and _h.Position or destPos
	end
	local _charAdded = false
	local _caConn = LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function() _charAdded = true end)
	local _cloneHoldConn
	local function _releaseCloneAnchor()
		if _cloneHoldConn then
			_cloneHoldConn:Disconnect()
			_cloneHoldConn = nil
		end
		local char = LP.Character
		local h = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if h then
			h.Anchored = false
			local bv = h:FindFirstChild("JewskiCarpetBV")
			if bv then bv:Destroy() end
		end
		if hum then
			hum.PlatformStand = false
				hum.AutoRotate = true
		end
	end
	local function _anchorForClone()
		local char = LP.Character
		local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if not char or not hum then return end
		if _cloneHoldConn then
			pcall(function() _cloneHoldConn:Disconnect() end)
			_cloneHoldConn = nil
		end
		local _face = (facingDir and facingDir.Magnitude > 0.1) and facingDir.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
		local wallCF = CFrame.new(destPos, destPos + _face)
		if hum then
			pcall(function()
				hum.AutoRotate = false
				hum.PlatformStand = true
			end)
		end
		_cloneHoldConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			local currentChar = LP.Character
			local rh = currentChar and currentChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not rh then return end
			local bv = rh:FindFirstChild("JewskiCarpetBV")
			if bv then pcall(function() bv:Destroy() end) end
			pcall(function()
				rh.CFrame = wallCF
				rh.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				rh.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end)
			local h = currentChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if h then
				h.AutoRotate = false
				h.PlatformStand = true
			end
		end)
	end

	_G.jewskiStealHold = false

	do
		local _face = (facingDir and facingDir.Magnitude > 0.1) and facingDir.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
		local wallCF = CFrame.new(destPos, destPos + _face)
		local _pinHum = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if _pinHum then pcall(function() _pinHum.AutoRotate = false end) end
		local _h = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if _h then
			pcall(function()
				_h.CFrame = wallCF
				_h.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				_h.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end)
		end
		RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
	end

	pcall(_anchorForClone)

	if not _G.jewskiTPStop then
		doClone()
	end
	if _clonePlat then
		local _plat = _clonePlat
		_clonePlat = nil
		task.delay(1.0, function() pcall(function() _plat:Destroy() end) end)
	end

	do
		local _t0 = os.clock()
		local _done = false
		repeat
			if _G.jewskiTPStop then break end
			if _charAdded then _done = true; break end
			if LP.Character ~= _preCloneChar then _done = true; break end
			local _h = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if _h and _preClonePos then
				local _dx = _h.Position.X - _preClonePos.X
				local _dz = _h.Position.Z - _preClonePos.Z
				if (_dx * _dx + _dz * _dz) > 2.25 then _done = true; break end
			end
			RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
		until os.clock() - _t0 > 0.45
	end
	if _caConn then pcall(function() _caConn:Disconnect() end) end
	_releaseCloneAnchor()
	_G.isCloning = false

	pcall(function()
		local _rh = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if _rh then _rh.AutoRotate = true end
	end)

	if _G.jewskiTPStop then endTP(); return end

	_G.Jewski_Step("clone done, goToBrainrot")

	_G._curStealSlot = tonumber(pet and pet.slot) or _G._curStealSlot

	do local d = tonumber(_G.JewskiPostCloneGoDelay) or 0.12; if d > 0 then task.wait(d) end end
	_boostGrabWindow(15)
	goToBrainrot(petPos, { fromTp = true, slot = pet and pet.slot })
	_boostGrabWindow(12)
	_G.isTeleporting = false
	isTeleporting = false
	if type(_G.JewskiInstantGrabNow) == "function" then
		task.defer(function()
			pcall(_G.JewskiInstantGrabNow, pet)
			task.wait(0.05)
			pcall(_G.JewskiInstantGrabNow, pet)
		end)
	end
	_VIM_TP:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.C, false, game)
		task.wait(0.05)
		_VIM_TP:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.C, false, game)

	if healConn then healConn:Disconnect() end
	endTP()
	end) 
	if not _tpOk then
		warn("[JEWSKI] doVelocityTP error:", _tpErr)
		pcall(endTP)
	end
	return _tpOk
end
_G.__VPRIV_doVelocityTP = doVelocityTP

_G.Jewski_IsInsideBase = function(plotName, petY)
if not plotName then return false end
local char = LP.Character
local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not hrp then return false end
local plotsFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
local plot = plotsFolder and plotsFolder:FindFirstChild(plotName)
if not plot then return false end
local ok, pp = pcall(function() return plot:GetPivot().Position end)
if not ok or not pp then return false end
local rad = (petY and petY <= 8.9) and 26 or 25
local p = hrp.Position
return math.abs(p.X - pp.X) < rad and math.abs(p.Z - pp.Z) < rad
end

task.spawn(function()
local _seenPriorityUIDs = {}
local _primed = false
while true do

local _priSet = {}

local currentPriority = {}
for _, p in ipairs(cache) do
if p.plot and p.slot then
local isPriority = _priSet[tostring(p.name or ""):lower()]
or _priSet[tostring(p.index or ""):lower()]
if isPriority then
local uid = p.uid or (tostring(p.plot) .. "_" .. tostring(p.slot))
currentPriority[uid] = p
end
end
end
if not _primed then
for uid in pairs(currentPriority) do _seenPriorityUIDs[uid] = true end
_primed = true
else
for uid, p in pairs(currentPriority) do
if not _seenPriorityUIDs[uid] then
_seenPriorityUIDs[uid] = true
manuallySelectedUID = uid
selectedTargetUID = uid
SharedState.SelectedPetData = p

if _G.Jewski_ExecuteManualTP then pcall(_G.Jewski_ExecuteManualTP)
elseif _G.JewskiStartSideTP then pcall(_G.JewskiStartSideTP) end
end)
end
end
for uid in pairs(_seenPriorityUIDs) do
if not currentPriority[uid] then _seenPriorityUIDs[uid] = nil end
end
end
end
end
end)

local function clampPos(x, y)
local vp = workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1920, 1080)
local w = _outer.AbsoluteSize.X
local h = _outer.AbsoluteSize.Y
x = math.clamp(x, 0, math.max(0, vp.X - math.min(w, 80)))
y = math.clamp(y, 0, math.max(0, vp.Y - math.min(h, 80)))
return x, y
end
local function savePos()
local abs = _outer.AbsolutePosition
local xo, yo = math.floor(abs.X + 0.5), math.floor(abs.Y + 0.5)
if not Config.JewskiPositions then Config.JewskiPositions = {} end
Config.JewskiPositions[_AS_KEY] = {xs=0, xo=xo, ys=0, yo=yo}
local ps = workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize
if not Config.Positions then Config.Positions = {} end
Config.Positions[_AS_KEY] = {X = xo / math.max(1, ps.X), Y = yo / math.max(1, ps.Y)}
if getgenv then
if type(getgenv().__JEWSKI_PanelPositions) ~= "table" then getgenv().__JEWSKI_PanelPositions = {} end
getgenv().__JEWSKI_PanelPositions[_AS_KEY] = Config.JewskiPositions[_AS_KEY]
getgenv().__JEWSKI_PanelPositions[_AS_SIZE_KEY] = Config.JewskiPositions[_AS_SIZE_KEY]
end
_G.__StealTargetUserMoved = true
pcall(SaveConfig)
end
local function beginDrag(input)
if _G._JEWSKISliderDragging then return end
if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
dragging = true
dragStart = input.Position
local abs = _outer.AbsolutePosition
startPos = Vector2.new(abs.X, abs.Y)
_outer.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(abs.X, abs.Y)
end
end
_header.InputBegan:Connect(beginDrag)
_bot.InputBegan:Connect(beginDrag)
UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
if not dragging then return end
if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
local d = input.Position - dragStart
local nx, ny = clampPos(startPos.X + d.X, startPos.Y + d.Y)
_outer.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(nx, ny)
end
end)
UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
if dragging then
dragging = false
savePos()
end
end
end)
end

_priBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
_priBtn.ZIndex = 51
Instance.new("UICorner", _priBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
local _nearBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", _modeRow)

_nearBtn.TextSize = 11
_nearBtn.TextColor3 = _C.DIM
_nearBtn.AutoButtonColor = false

local function _paintMode()
local isPri = tostring(_G.JewskiTPMode or _G.JewskiStealMode or "priority"):lower() ~= "nearest"
_priBtn.BackgroundColor3 = isPri and Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 250) or Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 26)
_priBtn.TextColor3 = isPri and Color3.fromRGB(10,10,12) or _C.DIM
_nearBtn.BackgroundColor3 = (not isPri) and Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 250) or Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 26)
_nearBtn.TextColor3 = (not isPri) and Color3.fromRGB(10,10,12) or _C.DIM
end

if type(_G.JewskiSetTgtMode) == "function" then pcall(_G.JewskiSetTgtMode, "priority") end
_paintMode()
end)
_nearBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
if type(_G.JewskiSetTgtMode) == "function" then pcall(_G.JewskiSetTgtMode, "nearest") end
_paintMode()
end)
_paintMode()
local _scroll=Instance.new("ScrollingFrame",_outer)
_scroll.Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,1,-110)
_scroll.Position=UDim2.new(0,6,0,90)
_scroll.BackgroundTransparency=1
_scroll.BorderSizePixel=0
_scroll.ScrollBarThickness=3
_scroll.ScrollBarImageColor3=_C.ACCENT
_scroll.BackgroundTransparency=1
_scroll.BorderSizePixel=0; _scroll.ScrollBarThickness=2
_scroll.ScrollBarImageColor3=_C.ACCENT; _scroll.CanvasSize=UDim2.new(0,0,0,0); _scroll.Active=true
local _sl=Instance.new("UIListLayout",_scroll)
_sl.Padding=UDim.new(0,2); _sl.SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
_sl:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
_scroll.CanvasSize=UDim2.new(0,0,0,_sl.AbsoluteContentSize.Y)
end)
local AS_SLOTS = 13
local _asSlots = {}
for i = 1, AS_SLOTS do
local row=Instance.new("TextButton",_scroll)
row.Name="Slot"..i; row.Size=UDim2.new(1,-4,0,28); row.LayoutOrder=i
row.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 20); row.BackgroundTransparency=0
row.BorderSizePixel=0; row.Text=""; row.AutoButtonColor=false
_c(row, 10)
local stroke=Instance.new("UIStroke",row)
stroke.Color=_C.GLOW; stroke.Thickness=1.1; stroke.Transparency=0.35; stroke.Enabled=false

bar.BackgroundColor3=_C.DIM; bar.BackgroundTransparency=1; bar.BorderSizePixel=0; _c(bar,1)

txt.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; txt.TextTruncate=Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd

gen.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold; gen.TextSize=9
gen.TextColor3=_C.GREEN; gen.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Right

function _asRefresh()
if _outer and (_outer.Parent==nil or _outer.Visible==false) then return end
local _n = os.clock()
if _G.__asRefLast and (_n - _G.__asRefLast) < 0.35 then return end
_G.__asRefLast = _n
local minV=0
local all = get_all_pets() or {}
local sig = tostring(#all)
for i = 1, math.min(8, #all) do
local p = all[i]
if p then sig = sig .. "|" .. tostring(p.uid or p.name or "") .. tostring(p.mpsValue or p.mps or "") end
end
if _G.__asRefSig == sig then return end
_G.__asRefSig = sig
local shown={}
local mode = tostring(_G.JewskiTPMode or _G.JewskiStealMode or "priority"):lower()
local nearestOn = mode == "nearest"
local hrp = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local manuallySelectedUID = _G.JewskiManualStealUID
if manuallySelectedUID then
local stillExists = false
for _, pet in ipairs(all) do
if pet.uid == manuallySelectedUID then
stillExists = true
break
end
end
if not stillExists then
_G.JewskiManualStealUID = nil
_G.JewskiStealTarget = nil
manuallySelectedUID = nil
end
end
if nearestOn then
local sorted = {}
for _, pet in ipairs(all) do
if (pet.mpsValue or 0) >= minV then sorted[#sorted+1] = pet end
end
table.sort(sorted, function(a, b)
local da, db = math.huge, math.huge
if hrp then
if a.position then da = (hrp.Position - a.position).Magnitude end
if b.position then db = (hrp.Position - b.position).Magnitude end
end
return da < db
end)
for i = 1, math.min(AS_SLOTS, #sorted) do shown[i] = sorted[i] end
else
local used = {}
local _priList = _G.SHARED_PRIORITY_ITEMS
if type(_priList) == "table" then
for _, pName in ipairs(_priList) do
local sn = tostring(pName):lower()
local matches = {}
for _, pet in ipairs(all) do
if not used[pet.uid] and (pet.mpsValue or 0) >= minV then
local n = (pet.petName or pet.name or ""):lower()
local idx = (pet.index or ""):lower()
if n == sn or idx == sn then
matches[#matches+1] = pet
end
end
end
table.sort(matches, function(a,b) return (a.mpsValue or 0) > (b.mpsValue or 0) end)
for _, pet in ipairs(matches) do
shown[#shown+1] = pet
used[pet.uid] = true
if #shown >= AS_SLOTS then break end
end
if #shown >= AS_SLOTS then break end
end
end
if #shown < AS_SLOTS then
local rest = {}
for _, pet in ipairs(all) do
if not used[pet.uid] and (pet.mpsValue or 0) >= minV then
rest[#rest+1] = pet
end
end
table.sort(rest, function(a,b) return (a.mpsValue or 0) > (b.mpsValue or 0) end)
for _, pet in ipairs(rest) do
shown[#shown+1] = pet
if #shown >= AS_SLOTS then break end
end
end
end
local selectedTargetUID = nil
if not manuallySelectedUID then
local pick = nil
if nearestOn then
pick = shown[1]
else
local _priList2 = _G.SHARED_PRIORITY_ITEMS
if type(_priList2) == "table" then
for _, pName in ipairs(_priList2) do
local sn = tostring(pName):lower()
for _, pet in ipairs(all) do
local n = (pet.petName or pet.name or ""):lower()
local idx = (pet.index or ""):lower()
if n == sn or idx == sn then pick = pet; break end
end
if pick then break end
end
end
if not pick then pick = shown[1] end
end
if pick and pick.uid then
selectedTargetUID = pick.uid
end
end
for i=1,AS_SLOTS do
local slot=_asSlots[i]; local pet=shown[i]; slot.pet=pet
if pet then
local mut=pet.mutation or "None"; local mc=_MCOL[mut] or _MCOL.None
local isSel=(selectedTargetUID==pet.uid); local isMan=(manuallySelectedUID==pet.uid)
local mutTxt=""
if mut~="None" then mutTxt=string.format('<font color="#%s">%s</font> ',_asHex(mc),_asEsc(mut)) end
slot.txt.Text=string.format('#%d: %s<b>%s</b>',i,mutTxt,_asEsc(pet.petName or "?"))
slot.txt.TextColor3=isSel and _C.TEXT or Color3.fromRGB(235,238,248)
slot.gen.Text=pet.mpsText or (function(v)
if v>=1e12 then return string.format("$%.1fT/s",v/1e12)
elseif v>=1e9 then return string.format("$%.1fB/s",v/1e9)
elseif v>=1e6 then return string.format("$%.1fM/s",v/1e6)
elseif v>=1e3 then return string.format("$%.1fK/s",v/1e3)
elseif v>0 then return string.format("$%.0f/s",v) else return "" end
end)(pet.mpsValue or 0)
slot.bar.BackgroundColor3=mc; slot.bar.BackgroundTransparency=0
slot.row.BackgroundColor3=isSel and _C.ROW_SEL or Color3.fromRGB(18,18,20)
slot.row.BackgroundTransparency=isSel and 0 or 0.15
slot.stroke.Color=isMan and _C.GREEN or _C.ACCENT
slot.stroke.Enabled=(isSel or isMan)
else
slot.txt.Text="empty"; slot.txt.TextColor3=_C.DIM
slot.gen.Text=""; slot.bar.BackgroundTransparency=1
slot.row.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(18,18,20); slot.row.BackgroundTransparency=0
slot.stroke.Enabled=false
end
end
end
_G._asRefresh = _asRefresh
local _grip = Instance.new("TextButton")
_grip.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14)
_grip.Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
_grip.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1)
_grip.BackgroundColor3 = _C.STROKE
_grip.BackgroundTransparency = 0
_grip.BorderSizePixel = 0
_grip.Text = ""
_grip.AutoButtonColor = false
_grip.ZIndex = 20
_grip.Parent = _outer
local _gc = Instance.new("UICorner", _grip)
_gc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
do
local _rDrag = false
local _rStart, _rBase = nil, nil
_grip.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
_rDrag = true
_rStart = inp.Position
_rBase = _outer.AbsoluteSize
end
end)
UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(inp)
if (inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) and _rDrag then
_rDrag = false
local sz = _outer.AbsoluteSize
if not Config.JewskiPositions then Config.JewskiPositions = {} end
Config.JewskiPositions[_AS_SIZE_KEY] = {w = sz.X, h = sz.Y}
SaveConfig()
end
end)
UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(inp)
if not _rDrag then return end
if inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
and inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
local d = inp.Position - _rStart
local newW = math.clamp(_rBase.X + d.X, 160, 500)
local newH = math.clamp(_rBase.Y + d.Y, 120, 700)
_outer.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(newW, newH)
end)
end
local _BASE_W, _BASE_H = 240, 400
local _BASE_ROW_H = 28
local _BASE_TXT_SIZE = 11
local _BASE_GEN_SIZE = 9
local _BASE_GEN_W = 70
local _BASE_GEN_XOFF = 74
local _BASE_TXT_XOFF = 12
local _BASE_BAR_H = 18
local function _applyRowSizes()
local sz = _outer.AbsoluteSize
if sz.X < 10 or sz.Y < 10 then return end
local scaleW = sz.X / _BASE_W
local scaleH = sz.Y / _BASE_H
local scale  = math.min(scaleW, scaleH)
local rowH    = math.clamp(math.floor(_BASE_ROW_H * scaleH + 0.5), 20, 80)
local txtSize = math.clamp(math.floor(_BASE_TXT_SIZE * scale + 0.5), 8, 22)
local genSize = math.clamp(math.floor(_BASE_GEN_SIZE * scale + 0.5), 7, 18)
local genW    = math.clamp(math.floor(_BASE_GEN_W * scaleW + 0.5), 50, 160)
local genXOff = math.clamp(math.floor(_BASE_GEN_XOFF * scaleW + 0.5), 40, 160)
local txtXOff = math.clamp(math.floor(_BASE_TXT_XOFF * scaleW + 0.5), 6, 30)
local barH    = math.clamp(math.floor(_BASE_BAR_H * scaleH + 0.5), 10, 50)
for _, slot in ipairs(_asSlots) do
pcall(function()
slot.row.Size     = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, rowH)
slot.txt.TextSize = txtSize
slot.txt.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(txtXOff, 0)
slot.txt.Size     = UDim2.new(1, -(genW + 18), 1, 0)
slot.gen.TextSize = genSize
slot.gen.Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(genW, rowH)
slot.gen.Position = UDim2.new(1, -genXOff, 0, 0)
slot.bar.Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(3, barH)
slot.bar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0.5, -math.floor(barH/2))
end)
end
end
_outer:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(_applyRowSizes)
_applyRowSizes()
task.spawn(function() while _outer.Parent do
task.wait(_G._isTpMoving and 4 or 3.0)
if not _G._isTpMoving then pcall(_asRefresh) end
end end)
pcall(_asRefresh)
end
end)
if type(refreshTargetPanel) ~= "function" then
function refreshTargetPanel()
if _G._asRefresh then pcall(_G._asRefresh) end
end
end
end)

local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")

sg.DisplayOrder = 999999
sg.Enabled = (_G.__JEWSKI_UI_HOLD ~= true)

local function _smartInteract(number)
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
local hrp = char and (char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso"))
if not char or not hrp then return end
local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
if not plots then return end
local closestPlot, minDist = nil, 40
for _, plot in pairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
local plotPos
if plot:IsA("Model") then
plotPos = plot.PrimaryPart and plot.PrimaryPart.Position or plot:GetPivot().Position
else
plotPos = plot.Position
end
local dist = (hrp.Position - plotPos).Magnitude
if dist < minDist then closestPlot = plot; minDist = dist end
end
if closestPlot and closestPlot:FindFirstChild("Unlock") then
local items = {}
for _, item in pairs(closestPlot.Unlock:GetChildren()) do
local pos = item:IsA("Model") and item:GetPivot().Position or item.Position
table.insert(items, { Obj = item, Y = pos.Y })
end
table.sort(items, function(a, b) return a.Y < b.Y end)
if items[number] then
for _, pr in pairs(items[number].Obj:GetDescendants()) do
if pr:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
pcall(function() fireproximityprompt(pr) end)
end
end
end
end
end

local function _petLabel(pet)
if not pet or type(pet) ~= "table" then return nil end
local mut = pet.mutation or pet.Mutation
local name = pet.petName or pet.name or pet.Name or "?"
if mut and tostring(mut) ~= "" and tostring(mut):lower() ~= "none" then
return tostring(mut) .. " " .. tostring(name)
end
return tostring(name)
end
local function _statusTargetName(st)
local n = _petLabel(st and st.target)
if n then return n end
n = _petLabel(_G.JewskiStealTarget)
if n then return n end
return "Targeting..."
end
local _barLast = 0
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
local _n = os.clock()
if _n - _barLast < 0.1 then return end
_barLast = _n
if _G.JewskiAutoSteal ~= true then
if hud.Visible then hud.Visible = false end; return
end
local st = _G.Jewski_StealStatus or {}
local name = _statusTargetName(st)
local st2 = _G.Jewski_StealStatus
if type(st2) == "table" and st2.active and st2.start then
    st = st2
end
if LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") then
    hud.Visible = true; openRow.Visible = false
    fill.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    pctLbl.Text = "100%"
    targetNameLbl.Text = name ~= "Targeting..." and ("HOLDING  ·  " .. name) or "HOLDING"
    fill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(55, 230, 120)
elseif st.active and st.start then
    hud.Visible = true; openRow.Visible = false
    local dur = tonumber(st.duration)
    if not dur or dur <= 0 then
        dur = tonumber(_G.JewskiStealHoldDuration) or 1.3
    end
    local p = (tick() - st.start) / dur
    p = math.clamp(p, 0, 1)
    fill.Size = UDim2.new(p, 0, 1, 0)
    pctLbl.Text = math.floor(p * 100 + 0.5) .. "%"
    targetNameLbl.Text = name
    if p >= 1 then
        fill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 200, 255) 
        pctLbl.Text = "100%"
        if (tick() - st.start) > (dur + 0.8) and not LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") then
            st.active = false
        end
    else
        fill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 220, 110)
    end
elseif st.target then
    hud.Visible = true; openRow.Visible = false
    fill.Size = UDim2.new(0.05, 0, 1, 0)
    pctLbl.Text = "..."
    targetNameLbl.Text = name
    fill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 190, 100)
else
    if hud.Visible then hud.Visible = false; openRow.Visible = false end
end
end)
end)
do
local _ctrlToggleAt = 0
game:GetService("UserInputService").InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
if input.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl then return end
local box
pcall(function() box = game:GetService("UserInputService"):GetFocusedTextBox() end)
if box then return end
if gameProcessed then return end
local now = os.clock()
if now - _ctrlToggleAt < 0.28 then return end
_ctrlToggleAt = now
if type(_G.Jewski_ToggleConfigMenu) == "function" then
pcall(_G.Jewski_ToggleConfigMenu)
end
end)
end
task.spawn(function()
LocalPlayer = LocalPlayer or game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer

task.wait(1)
pcall(function()
local pg = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
local old = pg:FindFirstChild("XiConfigPanel")
if old then old:Destroy() end
local old2 = pg:FindFirstChild("JewskiCtrlMenu")
if old2 then old2:Destroy() end
local BG      = Color3.fromRGB(10, 14, 10)
local PANEL   = Color3.fromRGB(14, 22, 14)
local ROW     = Color3.fromRGB(18, 30, 20)
local ROW_H   = Color3.fromRGB(26, 44, 28)
local ON      = Color3.fromRGB(55, 205, 105)
local OFF     = Color3.fromRGB(28, 46, 30)
local ACCENT  = Color3.fromRGB(55, 205, 105)
local STROKE  = Color3.fromRGB(38, 76, 44)
local TEXT    = Color3.fromRGB(240, 255, 242)
local DIM     = Color3.fromRGB(120, 158, 128)
local RED     = Color3.fromRGB(220, 65, 70)
local BLUE    = Color3.fromRGB(80, 140, 220)
local YELLOW  = Color3.fromRGB(240, 200, 60)
local FONT    = Enum.Font.GothamBold
local FONTSM  = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
local FONTBLK = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
local function mk(class, props, parent)
local i = Instance.new(class)
for k, v in pairs(props) do i[k] = v end
if parent then i.Parent = parent end
return i
end
local function cr(inst, r)
mk("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r or 6)}, inst)
end
local function st(inst, col, th, tr)
local s = mk("UIStroke", {Color = col or STROKE, Thickness = th or 1, Transparency = tr or 0.25}, inst)
return s
end
local sg = mk("ScreenGui", {
Name = "JewskiCtrlMenu", ResetOnSpawn = false, Enabled = (_G.__JEWSKI_UI_HOLD ~= true),
IgnoreGuiInset = true, DisplayOrder = 9996,
ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
}, pg)
local main = mk("Frame", {
Name = "CtrlMain",
Size = UDim2.fromOffset(420, 520),
Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -210, 0.5, -260),
BackgroundColor3 = BG,
BackgroundTransparency = 0.04,
BorderSizePixel = 0,
ClipsDescendants = false,
Visible = false,
ZIndex = 2,
}, sg)
cr(main, 14)
st(main, STROKE, 1.6, 0.2)
local glow = mk("ImageLabel", {
Name = "Glow", Size = UDim2.new(1, 60, 1, 60),
Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-30, -30),
BackgroundTransparency = 1,
Image = "rbxassetid://5028857084",
ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 140, 60),
ImageTransparency = 0.7,
ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice,
SliceCenter = Rect.new(24, 24, 276, 276),
ZIndex = 1,
}, main)
local header = mk("Frame", {
Name = "Header",
Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 42),
BackgroundColor3 = PANEL,
BackgroundTransparency = 0.1,
BorderSizePixel = 0,
ZIndex = 10,
}, main)
cr(header, 10)
mk("Frame", {
Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0),
Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0),
BackgroundColor3 = PANEL,
BackgroundTransparency = 0.1,
BorderSizePixel = 0,
ZIndex = 11,
}, header)
local htitle = mk("TextLabel", {
Size = UDim2.new(1, -80, 1, 0),
Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 0),
BackgroundTransparency = 1,
Text = "JEWSKI  ·  CTRL MENU",
Font = FONTBLK, TextSize = 15,
TextColor3 = TEXT,
TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
ZIndex = 12,
}, header)
mk("UIGradient", {
Color = ColorSequence.new({
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(120, 255, 160)),
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(100, 220, 140)),
}),
}, htitle)
local sub = mk("TextLabel", {
Size = UDim2.new(1, -80, 0, 12),
Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 26),
BackgroundTransparency = 1,
Text = "LeftCtrl to toggle  •  drag header to move",
Font = FONTSM, TextSize = 10,
TextColor3 = DIM,
TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
ZIndex = 12,
}, header)
local closeBtn = mk("TextButton", {
Size = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 26),
Position = UDim2.new(1, -32, 0.5, -13),
BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 30, 30),
AutoButtonColor = false,
Text = "✕", Font = FONTBLK, TextSize = 14,
TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 15,
}, header)
cr(closeBtn, 6)
closeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() main.Visible = false end)
closeBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() closeBtn.BackgroundColor3 = RED end)
closeBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() closeBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 30, 30) end)
do
local dragging, dragStart, startPos = false, nil, nil
header.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
if Config.UILocked then return end
if _G._JEWSKISliderDragging then return end
if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
dragging = true; dragStart = i.Position; startPos = main.Position
end
end)
UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
if not dragging or Config.UILocked or _G._JEWSKISliderDragging then return end
if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
local d = i.Position - dragStart
main.Position = UDim2.new(
startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X,
startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
end
end)
UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
dragging = false
end
end)
end
mk("Frame", {
Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 1),
Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 42),
BackgroundColor3 = STROKE,
BackgroundTransparency = 0.05,
BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 10,
}, main)
local TABS = {"STEAL", "TP", "ESP", "SETTINGS"}
local tabBar = mk("Frame", {
Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 32),
Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 48),
BackgroundTransparency = 1,
BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 10,
}, main)
mk("UIListLayout", {
FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,
SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
Padding = UDim.new(0, 4),
}, tabBar)
local contentFrame = mk("Frame", {
Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -92),
Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 86),
BackgroundTransparency = 1,
BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 5,
}, main)
local function makeScrollBody()
local sf = mk("ScrollingFrame", {
Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
BackgroundTransparency = 1,
BorderSizePixel = 0,
ScrollBarThickness = 3,
ScrollBarImageColor3 = ACCENT,
CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
Visible = false, ZIndex = 5,
}, contentFrame)
mk("UIListLayout", {
SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
Padding = UDim.new(0, 5),
}, sf)
mk("UIPadding", {
PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 4),
PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 8),
PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 2),
PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 2),
}, sf)
return sf
end
local _rowOrder = {}
local function sectionLabel(body, text)
local lbl = mk("TextLabel", {
Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18),
BackgroundTransparency = 1,
Text = "  " .. text:upper(),
Font = FONTBLK, TextSize = 10,
TextColor3 = DIM,
TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
ZIndex = 6,
}, body)
_rowOrder[body] = (_rowOrder[body] or 0) + 1
lbl.LayoutOrder = _rowOrder[body]
end
local function makeToggleRow(body, label, getV, setV, col)
_rowOrder[body] = (_rowOrder[body] or 0) + 1
local row = mk("Frame", {
Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34),
BackgroundColor3 = ROW,
BackgroundTransparency = 0,
BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 6,
LayoutOrder = _rowOrder[body],
}, body)
cr(row, 6)
local lbl = mk("TextLabel", {
Size = UDim2.new(1, -72, 1, 0),
Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 0),
BackgroundTransparency = 1,
Text = label, Font = FONT, TextSize = 12,
TextColor3 = TEXT,
TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, ZIndex = 7,
}, row)
local toggle = mk("TextButton", {
Size = UDim2.fromOffset(56, 24),
Position = UDim2.new(1, -62, 0.5, -12),
BackgroundColor3 = getV() and (col or ON) or OFF,
AutoButtonColor = false,
Text = getV() and "ON" or "OFF",
Font = FONTBLK, TextSize = 11,
TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 7,
}, row)
cr(toggle, 5)
local function refresh()
local v = getV()
toggle.BackgroundColor3 = v and (col or ON) or OFF
toggle.Text = v and "ON" or "OFF"
end
toggle.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
setV(not getV())
refresh()
pcall(SaveConfig)
if type(_G.JewskiSaveConfigNow) == "function" then pcall(_G.JewskiSaveConfigNow) end
end)
row.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
row.BackgroundColor3 = ROW_H
row.BackgroundTransparency = 0
end)
row.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
row.BackgroundColor3 = ROW
row.BackgroundTransparency = 0
end)
return refresh
end
local function makeButtonRow(body, label, callback, col)
_rowOrder[body] = (_rowOrder[body] or 0) + 1
local btn = mk("TextButton", {
Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34),
BackgroundColor3 = col or ROW,
BackgroundTransparency = 0.1,
AutoButtonColor = false,
Text = label, Font = FONTBLK, TextSize = 12,
TextColor3 = TEXT,
BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 6,
LayoutOrder = _rowOrder[body],
}, body)
cr(btn, 6)
btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
if callback then pcall(callback) end
end)
btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
btn.BackgroundColor3 = ROW_H
btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0
end)
btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
btn.BackgroundColor3 = col or ROW
btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
end)
return btn
end
local function makeSliderRow(body, label, minV, maxV, getV, setV, fmt, step)
_rowOrder[body] = (_rowOrder[body] or 0) + 1
local holder = mk("Frame", {
Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 52),
BackgroundColor3 = ROW,
BackgroundTransparency = 0,
BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 6,
LayoutOrder = _rowOrder[body],
}, body)
cr(holder, 6)
local lbl = mk("TextLabel", {
Size = UDim2.new(0.65, 0, 0, 20),
Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 4),
BackgroundTransparency = 1,
Text = label, Font = FONT, TextSize = 11,
TextColor3 = TEXT,
TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, ZIndex = 7,
}, holder)
local valLbl = mk("TextLabel", {
Size = UDim2.new(0.35, -10, 0, 20),
Position = UDim2.new(0.65, 0, 0, 4),
BackgroundTransparency = 1,
Text = "", Font = FONTBLK, TextSize = 11,
TextColor3 = ON,
TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, ZIndex = 7,
}, holder)
local bar = mk("Frame", {
Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 8),
Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 32),
BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 50, 34),
BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 7,
}, holder)
cr(bar, 4)
local fill = mk("Frame", {
Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0),
BackgroundColor3 = ACCENT,
BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 8,
}, bar)
cr(fill, 4)
local knob = mk("Frame", {
Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14),
AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0),
BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 9,
}, bar)
cr(knob, 7)
local function render(v)
local pct = math.clamp((v - minV) / math.max(1e-6, maxV - minV), 0, 1)
fill.Size = UDim2.new(pct, 0, 1, 0)
knob.Position = UDim2.new(pct, 0, 0.5, 0)
valLbl.Text = string.format(fmt or "%d", v)
end
render(getV())
local dragging = false
local function applyX(x)
local pct = math.clamp((x - bar.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, bar.AbsoluteSize.X), 0, 1)
local raw = minV + pct * (maxV - minV)
if step then raw = math.floor(raw / step + 0.5) * step end
raw = math.clamp(raw, minV, maxV)
setV(raw)
render(raw)
end
bar.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
dragging = true; _G._JEWSKISliderDragging = true; applyX(i.Position.X)
end
end)
knob.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
dragging = true; _G._JEWSKISliderDragging = true
end
end)
UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
if dragging and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
applyX(i.Position.X)
end
end)
UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
if (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) and dragging then
dragging = false; _G._JEWSKISliderDragging = false
pcall(SaveConfig)
if type(_G.JewskiSaveConfigNow) == "function" then pcall(_G.JewskiSaveConfigNow) end
end
end)
end
local function makeDualRow(body, labelA, cbA, labelB, cbB, colA, colB)
_rowOrder[body] = (_rowOrder[body] or 0) + 1
local row = mk("Frame", {
Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34),
BackgroundTransparency = 1,
BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 6,
LayoutOrder = _rowOrder[body],
}, body)
mk("UIListLayout", {
FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,
SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
Padding = UDim.new(0, 6),
}, row)
for _, info in ipairs({{labelA, cbA, colA}, {labelB, cbB, colB}}) do
local btn = mk("TextButton", {
Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -3, 1, 0),
BackgroundColor3 = info[3] or ROW,
BackgroundTransparency = 0.1,
AutoButtonColor = false,
Text = info[1], Font = FONTBLK, TextSize = 11,
TextColor3 = TEXT,
BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 7,
}, row)
cr(btn, 6)
btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
if info[2] then pcall(info[2]) end
end)
btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
btn.BackgroundColor3 = ROW_H; btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0
end)
btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
btn.BackgroundColor3 = info[3] or ROW; btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
end)
end
end
local function makeInputRow(body, label, getV, setV, placeholder)
_rowOrder[body] = (_rowOrder[body] or 0) + 1
local holder = mk("Frame", {
Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 52),
BackgroundColor3 = ROW,
BackgroundTransparency = 0,
BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 6,
LayoutOrder = _rowOrder[body],
}, body)
cr(holder, 6)
mk("TextLabel", {
Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 18),
Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 4),
BackgroundTransparency = 1,
Text = label, Font = FONT, TextSize = 11,
TextColor3 = DIM,
TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, ZIndex = 7,
}, holder)
local box = mk("TextBox", {
Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 22),
Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 24),
BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 18, 12),
BorderSizePixel = 0,
Text = tostring(getV() or ""),
PlaceholderText = placeholder or "",
PlaceholderColor3 = DIM,
Font = FONT, TextSize = 11,
TextColor3 = TEXT,
TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
ClearTextOnFocus = false, ZIndex = 7,
}, holder)
cr(box, 5)
st(box, STROKE, 1)
mk("UIPadding", {PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 6), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 6)}, box)
box.Focused:Connect(function() _G._JEWSKISliderDragging = true end)
box.FocusLost:Connect(function()
_G._JEWSKISliderDragging = false
setV(box.Text)
pcall(SaveConfig)
end)
end
local bodies = {}
for _, tab in ipairs(TABS) do
bodies[tab] = makeScrollBody()
end
do
local b = bodies["STEAL"]
sectionLabel(b, "AUTO STEAL")
makeToggleRow(b, "Auto Steal",
function() return _G.JewskiAutoSteal == true end,
function(v)
_G.JewskiAutoSteal = v and true or false
if Config then Config.AutoStealEnabled = _G.JewskiAutoSteal end
if _G.JewskiSetAutoStealRuntime then pcall(_G.JewskiSetAutoStealRuntime, _G.JewskiAutoSteal) end
if type(_G.JewskiAutoStealFn) == "function" then pcall(_G.JewskiAutoStealFn, _G.JewskiAutoSteal) end
if type(_G.JewskiSaveConfigNow) == "function" then pcall(_G.JewskiSaveConfigNow) end
end)
local _paintPri, _paintNear
_paintPri = makeToggleRow(b, "Priority Mode",
function() return tostring(_G.JewskiTPMode or _G.JewskiStealMode or "priority"):lower() ~= "nearest" end,
function(v)
if v then
if _G.JewskiSetTgtMode then pcall(_G.JewskiSetTgtMode, "priority") end
else
if _G.JewskiSetTgtMode then pcall(_G.JewskiSetTgtMode, "nearest") end
end
if _paintNear then _paintNear() end
pcall(SaveConfig)
end)
_paintNear = makeToggleRow(b, "Nearest Mode",
function() return tostring(_G.JewskiTPMode or _G.JewskiStealMode or "priority"):lower() == "nearest" end,
function(v)
if v then
if _G.JewskiSetTgtMode then pcall(_G.JewskiSetTgtMode, "nearest") end
else
if _G.JewskiSetTgtMode then pcall(_G.JewskiSetTgtMode, "priority") end
end
if _paintPri then _paintPri() end
pcall(SaveConfig)
end)
sectionLabel(b, "INVISIBLE STEAL")
makeToggleRow(b, "Invis Enabled",
function() return _G.invisibleStealEnabled == true end,
function(v)
if _G.toggleInvisibleSteal then
if (_G.invisibleStealEnabled == true) ~= v then
pcall(_G.toggleInvisibleSteal)
end
end
end, BLUE)
makeToggleRow(b, "Auto Invis on Steal",
function() return _G.AutoInvisDuringSteal == true end,
function(v)
_G.AutoInvisDuringSteal = v and true or false
if Config then Config.AutoInvisDuringSteal = _G.AutoInvisDuringSteal end
if not _G.AutoInvisDuringSteal then
if type(_G.JewskiInvisOff) == "function" then pcall(_G.JewskiInvisOff) end
end
pcall(SaveConfig)
end)
makeToggleRow(b, "Auto Recover Lagback",
function() return Config and Config.AutoRecoverLagback ~= false end,
function(v)
if Config then Config.AutoRecoverLagback = v end
_G.AutoRecoverLagback = v
end)
makeSliderRow(b, "Invis Rotation", 0, 360,
function() return math.clamp(tonumber((Config and Config.InvisStealAngle) or _G.InvisStealAngle or 225), 0, 360) end,
function(v) v = math.floor(v + 0.5); _G.InvisStealAngle = v; if Config then Config.InvisStealAngle = v end end,
"%d°", 1)
makeSliderRow(b, "Sink Depth", 0, 18,
function() return math.clamp(tonumber((Config and Config.SinkSliderValue) or _G.SinkSliderValue or 7), 0, 18) end,
function(v) v = math.floor(v * 10 + 0.5) / 10; _G.SinkSliderValue = v; if Config then Config.SinkSliderValue = v end end,
"%.1f", 0.5)
sectionLabel(b, "AUTO KICK")
makeToggleRow(b, "Auto Kick on Steal",
function() return _G.JewskiAutoKick == true end,
function(v)
_G.JewskiAutoKick = v
if type(_G.JewskiSetAutoKick) == "function" then pcall(_G.JewskiSetAutoKick, v) end
end, RED)
makeToggleRow(b, "Kick to Private Server",
function() return _G.JewskiKickToPS == true end,
function(v) _G.JewskiKickToPS = v end, YELLOW)
makeInputRow(b, "PS Code / Link",
function() return _G.JewskiPSCode or "" end,
function(v)
_G.JewskiPSCode = (v or ""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
end, "https://... or code")
makeButtonRow(b, "Go to Private Server", function()
if type(_G.JewskiDoKick) == "function" then pcall(_G.JewskiDoKick, false) end
end, Color3.fromRGB(55, 80, 160))
sectionLabel(b, "HIT RECOVER")
makeToggleRow(b, "Back To Pet On Hit",
function() return _G.JewskiBackToPetOnHit ~= false end,
function(v)
_G.JewskiBackToPetOnHit = v and true or false
pcall(SaveConfig)
end)
makeToggleRow(b, "Grief Detector",
function() return _G.JewskiGriefDetector ~= false end,
function(v)
_G.JewskiGriefDetector = v and true or false
pcall(SaveConfig)
end)
sectionLabel(b, "ANTI EFFECTS")
makeToggleRow(b, "Anti Boogie",
function() return _G.JewskiAntiBoogie ~= false end,
function(v)
_G.JewskiAntiBoogie = v and true or false
pcall(SaveConfig)
end)
sectionLabel(b, "STEAL TRACKER")
makeToggleRow(b, "Steal Tracker",
function() return _G.JewskiStealTracker == true end,
function(v)
_G.JewskiStealTracker = v and true or false
pcall(SaveConfig)
end)
makeSliderRow(b, "Tracker Min Gen", 0, 100,
function()
local g = tonumber(_G.JewskiStealTrackerMinGen) or 0
if g >= 1e9 then return math.clamp(g / 1e9, 0, 100) end
if g >= 1e6 then return math.clamp(g / 1e6, 0, 100) end
return 0
end,
function(v)
v = math.floor(v + 0.5)
_G.JewskiStealTrackerMinGen = v * 1e6
pcall(SaveConfig)
end, "%dM/s", 1)
sectionLabel(b, "NAVIGATION")
makeDualRow(b, "Priority List",
function()
if type(_G.Jewski_TogglePriorityMenu) == "function" then
pcall(_G.Jewski_TogglePriorityMenu)
else
warn("[JEWSKI] Priority menu missing")
end
end,
"Steal Target",
function()
local pg2 = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
local j = pg2 and pg2:FindFirstChild("JewskiAutoStealPanel")
if j then j.Enabled = not j.Enabled end
end)
makeButtonRow(b, "Spam Owner Panel", function()
if _G.Jewski_OpenSpamOwner then pcall(_G.Jewski_OpenSpamOwner)
elseif _G.Jewski_ToggleSpamOwnerPanel then pcall(_G.Jewski_ToggleSpamOwnerPanel) end
end)
end
do
local b = bodies["TP"]
sectionLabel(b, "TELEPORT")
makeToggleRow(b, "TP on Load",
function() return Config and Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.TpOnLoad ~= false end,
function(v) if Config and Config.TpSettings then Config.TpSettings.TpOnLoad = v end end)
makeButtonRow(b, "Manual TP (T)", function()
if _G.Jewski_ExecuteManualTP then task.spawn(function() pcall(_G.Jewski_ExecuteManualTP) end) end
end, Color3.fromRGB(40, 90, 160))
makeButtonRow(b, "Instant Clone (V)", function()
task.spawn(function() pcall(_G.JewskiInstantClone) end)
end, Color3.fromRGB(60, 80, 140))
sectionLabel(b, "TP SPEEDS")
makeSliderRow(b, "Grabble TP Speed", 50, 600,
function() return tonumber((Config and Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.GrabbleTPSpeed) or 230) end,
function(v)
v = math.floor(v + 0.5)
if Config and Config.TpSettings then Config.TpSettings.GrabbleTPSpeed = v end
if type(_G.Jewski_SaveTpSettings) == "function" then pcall(_G.Jewski_SaveTpSettings) end
if type(_G.JewskiSaveConfigNow) == "function" then pcall(_G.JewskiSaveConfigNow) end
if _G.JewskiSetCarpetSpeed then pcall(_G.JewskiSetCarpetSpeed, v) end
_G.TPVelocity = v
if _G.Jewski_SyncTpSpeeds then pcall(_G.Jewski_SyncTpSpeeds) end
_G.JewskiCarpetSpeed = v
if _G.JewskiSetCarpetSpeed then pcall(_G.JewskiSetCarpetSpeed, v) end
end, "%d st/s", 5)
makeSliderRow(b, "Walk to Brainrot Speed", 30, 400,
function() return tonumber((Config and Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.WalkTPSpeed) or 190) end,
function(v)
v = math.floor(v + 0.5)
if Config and Config.TpSettings then Config.TpSettings.WalkTPSpeed = v end
if type(_G.Jewski_SaveTpSettings) == "function" then pcall(_G.Jewski_SaveTpSettings) end

local currentTab = nil
local tabBtns = {}
local function switchTab(tabName)
currentTab = tabName
for _, tab in ipairs(TABS) do
if bodies[tab] then bodies[tab].Visible = (tab == tabName) end
if tabBtns[tab] then
tabBtns[tab].BackgroundColor3 = (tab == tabName) and ACCENT or OFF
tabBtns[tab].TextColor3 = (tab == tabName) and Color3.new(0, 0, 0) or DIM
tabBtns[tab].BackgroundTransparency = (tab == tabName) and 0 or 0.1
end
end
end
for i, tabName in ipairs(TABS) do
local btn = mk("TextButton", {
Size = UDim2.new(0.25, -4, 1, 0),
BackgroundColor3 = OFF,
BackgroundTransparency = 0.1,
AutoButtonColor = false,
Text = tabName, Font = FONTBLK, TextSize = 11,
TextColor3 = DIM,
BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 11,
LayoutOrder = i,
}, tabBar)
cr(btn, 6)
tabBtns[tabName] = btn
btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() switchTab(tabName) end)
end
switchTab("STEAL")
_G.Jewski_ToggleConfigMenu = function()
local sg = main and main.Parent
if sg and sg:IsA("ScreenGui") then
if not sg.Enabled then sg.Enabled = true end
end
if main then
main.Visible = not main.Visible
end
end
_G.Jewski_ShowConfigMenu = function()
local sg = main and main.Parent
if sg and sg:IsA("ScreenGui") then sg.Enabled = true end
if main then main.Visible = true end
end
end)
end)
task.spawn(function()
Config = Config or _G.JewskiUIConfig or {}
_G.JewskiUIConfig = Config
task.wait(0.8)
pcall(function()
local LP = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local pg = LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") or LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
local old = pg:FindFirstChild("JewskiMiniStealInvis")
if old then old:Destroy() end
local C = {
BG = Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 12),
HEADER = Color3.fromRGB(16, 16, 18),
ROW = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 20),
ROW_SEL = Color3.fromRGB(32, 32, 36),
STROKE = Color3.fromRGB(55, 55, 60),
ACCENT = Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 245),
GREEN = Color3.fromRGB(200, 255, 220),
GREEN_D = Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 32),
TEXT = Color3.fromRGB(250, 250, 252),
DIM = Color3.fromRGB(140, 140, 148),
RED = Color3.fromRGB(255, 95, 105),
OFF = Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 26),
}
local function corner(f, r)
local u = Instance.new("UICorner", f)
u.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r or 6)
end
local function stroke(f, col, th)
local u = Instance.new("UIStroke", f)
u.Color = col or C.STROKE
u.Thickness = th or 1
u.Transparency = 0.3
end
local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
gui.Name = "JewskiMiniStealInvis"
gui.Enabled = true
gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
gui.DisplayOrder = 9991
gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
gui.Parent = (gethui and gethui()) or pg
local outer = Instance.new("Frame")
outer.Name = "MiniOuter"
outer.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(230, 420)
outer.BackgroundColor3 = C.BG
outer.BackgroundTransparency = 0
outer.BorderSizePixel = 0
outer.Active = true
outer.ClipsDescendants = true
outer.Parent = gui
corner(outer, 10)
stroke(outer, C.STROKE, 1.5)
do
local vp = workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1920, 1080)
local defX = 12
local defY = 100
local key = "MiniStealInvis"
local d = nil
local x, y = defX, defY
if false and d and type(d.xo) == "number" and type(d.yo) == "number" then
x, y = d.xo, d.yo
if y < 40 or y > vp.Y - 80 or x < -50 or x > vp.X - 40 then
x, y = defX, defY
end
end
outer.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y)
end
local header = Instance.new("TextButton", outer)
header.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 28)
header.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 5)
header.BackgroundColor3 = C.HEADER
header.BackgroundTransparency = 0
header.BorderSizePixel = 0
header.Text = "  STEAL + INVIS"
header.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
header.TextSize = 13
header.TextColor3 = C.TEXT
header.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
header.AutoButtonColor = false
corner(header, 6)
stroke(header, C.STROKE, 1)
do
local dragging, dragStart, startPos = false, nil, nil
header.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
dragging = true
dragStart = inp.Position
startPos = outer.Position
end
end)
UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(inp)
if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
if dragging then
dragging = false
if Config then
Config.JewskiPositions = Config.JewskiPositions or {}
Config.JewskiPositions["MiniStealInvis"] = {
xo = outer.Position.X.Offset,
yo = outer.Position.Y.Offset,
}
pcall(function()
if saveConfig then saveConfig() elseif SaveConfig then SaveConfig() end
end)
end
end
end
end)
UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(inp)
if not dragging then return end
if inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
and inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
local d = inp.Position - dragStart
outer.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(
startPos.X.Offset + d.X,
startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y
)
end)
end
local scroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame", outer)
scroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, -40)
scroll.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 36)
scroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1
scroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
scroll.ScrollBarThickness = 3
scroll.ScrollBarImageColor3 = C.ACCENT
scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
scroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
local list = Instance.new("UIListLayout", scroll)
list.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)
list.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
local function section(title)
local l = Instance.new("TextLabel", scroll)
l.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 18)
l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
l.Text = title
l.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
l.TextSize = 11
l.TextColor3 = C.DIM
l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
return l
end
local function makeToggle(label, getOn, setOn)
local b = Instance.new("TextButton", scroll)
b.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 30)
b.BorderSizePixel = 0
b.AutoButtonColor = false
b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
b.TextSize = 12
b.TextColor3 = C.TEXT
b.BackgroundTransparency = 0
corner(b, 8)
local st = Instance.new("UIStroke", b)
st.Thickness = 1
st.Transparency = 0.45
local function paint()
local on = getOn() and true or false
b.BackgroundColor3 = on and C.GREEN or C.OFF
b.TextColor3 = on and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or C.DIM
st.Color = on and (C.ON_STROKE or Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 230)) or C.STROKE
st.Transparency = on and 0.25 or 0.55
b.Text = label .. ": " .. (on and "ON" or "OFF")
end
paint()
b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
local cur = getOn() and true or false
setOn(not cur)
paint()
pcall(function()
if saveConfig then saveConfig() elseif SaveConfig then SaveConfig() end
end)
end)
return b, paint
end
local function makeSlider(label, minV, maxV, getV, setV)
local holder = Instance.new("Frame", scroll)

holder.BackgroundColor3 = C.ROW
holder.BackgroundTransparency = 0
holder.BorderSizePixel = 0
corner(holder, 6)

lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
lbl.TextSize = 11
lbl.TextColor3 = C.DIM
lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
local box = Instance.new("TextBox", holder)
box.Size = UDim2.new(0.4, -8, 0, 18)
box.Position = UDim2.new(0.58, 0, 0, 2)
box.BackgroundColor3 = C.HEADER
box.BorderSizePixel = 0
box.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
box.TextSize = 11
box.TextColor3 = C.TEXT
box.ClearTextOnFocus = false
corner(box, 4)
local bar = Instance.new("TextButton", holder)
bar.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 14)
bar.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 28)
bar.BackgroundColor3 = C.HEADER
bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
bar.Text = ""
bar.AutoButtonColor = false
corner(bar, 4)
local fill = Instance.new("Frame", bar)
fill.BackgroundColor3 = C.GREEN
fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
fill.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0)
corner(fill, 4)
local function round1(v)
return math.floor((tonumber(v) or 0) * 10 + 0.5) / 10
end
local function paint()
local v = tonumber(getV()) or minV
v = math.clamp(round1(v), minV, maxV)
local t = (v - minV) / math.max(1e-6, (maxV - minV))
fill.Size = UDim2.new(t, 0, 1, 0)
lbl.Text = string.format("%s: %.1f", label, v)
box.Text = string.format("%.1f", v)
end
paint()
local function apply(raw)
local v = tonumber(raw)
if not v then paint() return end
v = math.clamp(round1(v), minV, maxV)
setV(v)
paint()
pcall(function()
if saveConfig then saveConfig() elseif SaveConfig then SaveConfig() end
end)
end
box.FocusLost:Connect(function()
apply(box.Text)
end)
local function fromX(x)
local abs = bar.AbsolutePosition.X
local w = bar.AbsoluteSize.X
local t = 0
if w > 0 then t = math.clamp((x - abs) / w, 0, 1) end
apply(minV + t * (maxV - minV))
end
bar.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
fromX(inp.Position.X)
local move, up
move = UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i2)
if i2.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
or i2.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
fromX(i2.Position.X)
end
end)
up = UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(i2)
if i2.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
or i2.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
if move then move:Disconnect() end
if up then up:Disconnect() end
end
end)
end
end)
return holder
end
local function makeBtn(label, cb, col)
local b = Instance.new("TextButton", scroll)
b.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 30)
b.BackgroundColor3 = col or C.ROW
b.BackgroundTransparency = 0
b.BorderSizePixel = 0
b.AutoButtonColor = false
b.Text = label
b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
b.TextSize = 12
b.TextColor3 = C.TEXT
corner(b, 8)
local st = Instance.new("UIStroke", b)
st.Color = C.STROKE
st.Thickness = 1
st.Transparency = 0.45
b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
if cb then pcall(cb) end
end)
return b
end

task.spawn(function()

local painel
local function escolher(mode, alvo)
_G.JewskiFaceAwayBaseOwner = (mode == "baseowner")
_G.JewskiFaceAwayNearest = (mode == "nearest")
_G.JewskiFaceAwayTarget = (mode == "player") and tostring(alvo or "") or ""
pcall(function() if type(SaveConfig) == "function" then SaveConfig() end end)
end
local function currentModeId()
if _G.JewskiFaceAwayNearest == true then return "nearest" end
if type(_G.JewskiFaceAwayTarget) == "string" and _G.JewskiFaceAwayTarget ~= "" then return "player" end
return "baseowner"
end
local function ensurePanel()
if painel and painel.Parent then return painel end
local pg = eu:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or eu:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 5)
if not pg then return nil end
local old = pg:FindFirstChild("JewskiFaceAwayPanel")
if old then old:Destroy() end
local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
sg.Name = "JewskiFaceAwayPanel"
sg.ResetOnSpawn = false
sg.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
sg.DisplayOrder = 120
sg.Parent = (gethui and gethui()) or pg

painel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(236, 300)
painel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 21)
painel.BorderSizePixel = 0
painel.Active = true

Instance.new("UICorner", painel).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)

st.Color = Color3.fromRGB(38, 38, 46)
st.Thickness = 1
do
local key = "JEWSKI Face Away"
local d = Config.JewskiPositions and Config.JewskiPositions[key]
local vp = workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1920, 1080)
if d and type(d.xo) == "number" and type(d.yo) == "number" then
painel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(
math.clamp(d.xo, 0, math.max(0, vp.X - 80)),
math.clamp(d.yo, 40, math.max(40, vp.Y - 80))
)
else
painel.Position = UDim2.new(1, -266, 0, 190)
end
end
local title = Instance.new("TextLabel", painel)
title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 36)
title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 4)
title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
title.Text = "FACE AWAY"

title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
title.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
title.Active = true
do
local dragging, dragStart, startPos = false, nil, nil
title.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
if Config.UILocked then return end
if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
dragging = true
dragStart = input.Position
local abs = painel.AbsolutePosition
startPos = Vector2.new(abs.X, abs.Y)
painel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(abs.X, abs.Y)
end
end)
UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i2)
if not dragging then return end
if i2.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i2.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
local d = i2.Position - dragStart
local nx = startPos.X + d.X
local ny = startPos.Y + d.Y
local vp = workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1920, 1080)
nx = math.clamp(nx, 0, math.max(0, vp.X - 60))
ny = math.clamp(ny, 20, math.max(20, vp.Y - 60))
painel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(nx, ny)
end
end)
UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(i2)
if i2.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i2.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
if dragging then
dragging = false
if not Config.JewskiPositions then Config.JewskiPositions = {} end
local abs = painel.AbsolutePosition
Config.JewskiPositions["JEWSKI Face Away"] = {xs=0, xo=abs.X, ys=0, yo=abs.Y}
pcall(function() if type(SaveConfig) == "function" then SaveConfig() end end)
end
end
end)
end

if pl ~= eu then table.insert(list, pl) end
end
table.sort(list, function(a,b) return a.DisplayName:lower() < b.DisplayName:lower() end)
for _, pl in ipairs(list) do
local row = Instance.new("TextButton", scroll)
row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 22)
row.BackgroundColor3 = (_G.JewskiFaceAwayTarget == pl.Name) and Color3.fromRGB(60,60,70) or Color3.fromRGB(32,32,38)

row.TextSize = 11

row.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
row.BorderSizePixel = 0
Instance.new("UICorner", row).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
escolher("player", pl.Name)
rebuildPlayers()
end)
end
end
rebuildPlayers()
Plrs.PlayerAdded:Connect(function() task.defer(rebuildPlayers) end)
Plrs.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(pl)
if _G.JewskiFaceAwayTarget == pl.Name then
escolher("baseowner", nil)
end
task.defer(rebuildPlayers)
end)

local function slider(label, key, minV, maxV, step)
local holder = Instance.new("Frame", scroll)
holder.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 48)
holder.BackgroundColor3 = ROW
holder.BorderSizePixel = 0
Instance.new("UICorner", holder).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

local track = Instance.new("Frame", holder)
track.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 6)
