fill.Size = UDim2.new(math.clamp((v0 - minV) / (maxV - minV), 0, 1), 0, 1, 0)
fill.BackgroundColor3 = ACCENT
fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
Instance.new("UICorner", fill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", track)
btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
btn.Text = ""
local function setFromX(px)
local rel = math.clamp((px - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / track.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 1)
local v = minV + rel * (maxV - minV)
v = math.floor(v / step + 0.5) * step
v = math.clamp(v, minV, maxV)
Config.TpSettings[key] = v
fill.Size = UDim2.new(rel, 0, 1, 0)
lbl.Text = label .. ": " .. tostring(v)
end
local dragging = false
btn.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
dragging = true
setFromX(inp.Position.X)
end
end)
game:GetService("UserInputService").InputChanged:Connect(function(inp)
if not dragging then return end
if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
setFromX(inp.Position.X)
end
end)
game:GetService("UserInputService").InputEnded:Connect(function(inp)
if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
if dragging then
if type(_G.Jewski_SaveTpSettings) == "function" then _G.Jewski_SaveTpSettings() end
end
dragging = false
end
end)
end
local function toggle(label, key)
local b = Instance.new("TextButton", scroll)
b.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 32)
b.BackgroundColor3 = ROW
b.BorderSizePixel = 0
b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
b.TextSize = 12
b.TextColor3 = TEXT
b.AutoButtonColor = false
Instance.new("UICorner", b).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
local function paint()
local on = Config.TpSettings[key] and true or false
b.Text = label .. ": " .. (on and "ON" or "OFF")
b.BackgroundColor3 = on and Color3.fromRGB(35, 60, 45) or ROW
end
paint()
b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
Config.TpSettings[key] = not Config.TpSettings[key]
paint()
if type(_G.Jewski_SaveTpSettings) == "function" then _G.Jewski_SaveTpSettings() end
end)
end
local function section(title)
local l = Instance.new("TextLabel", scroll)
l.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 18)
l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
l.Text = title
l.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
l.TextSize = 10
l.TextColor3 = DIM
l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
end
section("SPEED")
slider("Grapple/Fly Speed", "GrabbleTPSpeed", 100, 1500, 10)
slider("Walk Speed", "WalkTPSpeed", 50, 600, 10)
slider("Close Base Speed", "FlyTPCloseSpeed", 20, 400, 5)
slider("Close Base Dist", "NearDist", 40, 250, 5)
section("TIMING")
slider("Clone Delay", "CloneDelayVal", 0, 1, 0.01)
slider("Grapple Duration", "GrappleDuration", 1, 8, 0.5)
section("BEHAVIOR")
toggle("TP on Load", "TpOnLoad")
toggle("Brainrot Carpet", "BrainrotCarpet")
_G.Jewski_ToggleTpSettings = function()
outer.Visible = not outer.Visible
end
end)
;(function()
Workspace = Workspace or workspace
Players = Players or game:GetService("Players")
Config = Config or _G.JewskiUIConfig or {}
_G.JewskiUIConfig = Config
local hidden = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
local destroyed = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
local conn = nil
local enabled = false
local KILL_NAMES = {
"craftingmachine","crafting","forge","fuse","duel","trade","machine","spin","wheel",
"event","portal","npc","vendor","futbol","livematches","live matches","shop","trader","plaza",
"phantom","robux","admin","collectzone","collect zone","restock","disco","bee","boggie","minigame",
"arcade","slotmachine","gacha","lucky","prize","balloon","firework","particle","vfx","fx_",
}
local SAFE = {"Plots","RenderedMovingAnimals","Camera","Characters","Terrain","Clouds"}
local function isOurs(inst)
if not inst then return true end
local n = inst.Name
return n == "JewskiLoadingPlacebo" or n == "Jewski_StealBar" or n == "ProximityAPCircle"
or n == "MynxxClonePlatform" or n == "AutoKickGui" or n == "JewskiMiniNotif"
or n == "VonInvisStealGui" or n == "JewskiClonePlatform" or n == "JewskiLoadingPlacebo"
or n == "Jewski_StealBar" or n == "Jewski_CornerDecal" or n == "JewskiPerfHud"
or n == "JewskiLineToBase" or n == "JewskiBrainrotBeam" or n == "JewskiNotif"
or n == "JewskiTempPlatform" or n == "JewskiHub_V3" or n == "JewskiMiniStealInvis"
end
local function underSafe(inst)
local p = inst
while p and p ~= Workspace do
for _, sa in ipairs(SAFE) do
if p.Name == sa then return true end
end
if Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(p) then return true end
p = p.Parent
end
return false
end
local function matchesKill(inst)
if isOurs(inst) or underSafe(inst) then return false end
local p = inst
while p and p ~= Workspace do
local nl = string.lower(tostring(p.Name or ""))
for _, kw in ipairs(KILL_NAMES) do
if string.find(nl, kw, 1, true) then return true end
end
p = p.Parent
end
return false
end
local function inCenter(inst)
local ok, pos = pcall(function()
if inst:IsA("BasePart") then return inst.Position end
if inst:IsA("Model") then
local ok2, cf = pcall(function() return inst:GetPivot() end)
if ok2 and cf then return cf.Position end
end
return nil
end)
if not ok or not pos then return false end
return pos.X >= -480 and pos.X <= -340 and pos.Z >= -130 and pos.Z <= 260
end
local function process(obj)
if not obj or not obj.Parent or isOurs(obj) or underSafe(obj) then return end
if obj:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or obj:IsA("Fire") or obj:IsA("Smoke") or obj:IsA("Sparkles")
or obj:IsA("Trail") or obj:IsA("Beam") or obj:IsA("Sound") or obj:IsA("PointLight")
or obj:IsA("SpotLight") or obj:IsA("SurfaceLight") or obj:IsA("BillboardGui") or obj:IsA("SurfaceGui") then
if matchesKill(obj) or inCenter(obj) then
if not destroyed[obj] then
destroyed[obj] = true
pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
end
end
return
end
if obj:IsA("Model") and matchesKill(obj) and inCenter(obj) then
if not destroyed[obj] then
destroyed[obj] = true
pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
end
return
end
if obj:IsA("BasePart") and matchesKill(obj) and inCenter(obj) then
if not hidden[obj] then
hidden[obj] = {
t = obj.Transparency,
cc = obj.CanCollide,
cq = obj.CanQuery,
ct = obj.CanTouch,
}
end
pcall(function()
obj.Transparency = 1
obj.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
obj.CanCollide = false
obj.CanQuery = false
obj.CanTouch = false
end)
elseif (obj:IsA("Decal") or obj:IsA("Texture")) and matchesKill(obj) and inCenter(obj) then
pcall(function() obj.Transparency = 1 end)
end
end
local function sweep()
if _G.__JEWSKI_LIGHT_MODE then return end
local list = Workspace:GetDescendants()
for i, o in ipairs(list) do
if not enabled then break end
process(o)
if i % 60 == 0 then task.wait() end
end
end
local function restore()
for part, data in pairs(hidden) do
pcall(function()
if part and part.Parent then
if type(data) == "table" then
part.Transparency = data.t or 0
if data.cc ~= nil then part.CanCollide = data.cc end
if data.cq ~= nil then part.CanQuery = data.cq end
if data.ct ~= nil then part.CanTouch = data.ct end
else
part.Transparency = data
end
pcall(function() part.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0 end)
end
end)
end
table.clear(hidden)
end
_G.setHideCenter = function(on)
enabled = on and true or false
if Config then Config.HideCenterStructures = enabled end
pcall(function()
if setToggle then setToggle("Hide Center Structures", enabled, true) end
end)
pcall(function() if saveConfig then saveConfig() elseif SaveConfig then SaveConfig() end end)
if conn then pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end); conn = nil end
if enabled then
task.spawn(sweep)
task.spawn(function()
task.wait(5)
if enabled then sweep() end
end)
conn = Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
if not enabled then return end
task.defer(function() process(obj) end)
end)
else
restore()
end
end
_G.Jewski_SetHideCenter = _G.setHideCenter
task.defer(function()
task.wait(2)
pcall(function()
_G.JewskiHideCenter = true
if Config then Config.HideCenterStructures = true end
if _G.setHideCenter then _G.setHideCenter(true) end
end)
end)
pcall(function()
local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
for _, n in ipairs({"Bloom","Blur","DepthOfField","SunRays","ColorCorrection","DiscoEffect","BeeBlur"}) do
local fx = Lighting:FindFirstChild(n)
if fx then pcall(function() fx.Enabled = false end) end
end
end)
end)()
;(function()
LocalPlayer = LocalPlayer or game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
Config = Config or _G.JewskiUIConfig or {}
_G.JewskiUIConfig = Config
if getgenv().__JEWSKI_ARConns then
for _, c in pairs(getgenv().__JEWSKI_ARConns) do
pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
end
end
getgenv().__JEWSKI_ARConns = {}
local function _regConn(c)
local t = getgenv().__JEWSKI_ARConns
t[#t + 1] = c
return c
end
local antiRagdollConnections = {}
local antiRagdollCharacter, antiRagdollHumanoid, antiRagdollRootPart, antiRagdollAnimator
local lastVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
local velocityChangeThreshold = 40
local velocityMagnitudeThreshold = 25
local maxVelocity = 15
local _arHardenConn, _arHealthConn, _arDiedConn
local function _hardHum(hum)
if not hum or not hum.Parent then return end
pcall(function() hum.BreakJointsOnDeath = false end)
pcall(function() hum.RequiresNeck       = false end)
pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead,        false) end)
pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll,     false) end)
pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false) end)
pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics,     false) end)
end
local function _softHum(hum)
if not hum or not hum.Parent then return end
pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead,        true) end)
pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll,     true) end)
pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, true) end)
pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics,     true) end)
pcall(function() hum.BreakJointsOnDeath = true end)
end
local function _reviveHum(hum)
if not hum or not hum.Parent then return end
pcall(function() hum.Health = hum.MaxHealth end)
pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) end)
end
local function _bindHardener(char)
if _arHardenConn then pcall(function() _arHardenConn:Disconnect() end); _arHardenConn = nil end
if _arHealthConn then pcall(function() _arHealthConn:Disconnect() end); _arHealthConn = nil end
if _arDiedConn   then pcall(function() _arDiedConn:Disconnect()   end); _arDiedConn   = nil end
local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not hum then return end
if _G.AntiRagdollEnabled then _hardHum(hum) end
_arHealthConn = _regConn(hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Health"):Connect(function()
if not (_G.AntiRagdollEnabled or _G.antiKnockbackEnabled) then return end
if _G.Jewski_IsResetting then return end
if hum.Health <= 0 then _reviveHum(hum) end
end))
_arDiedConn = _regConn(hum.Died:Connect(function()
if not (_G.AntiRagdollEnabled or _G.antiKnockbackEnabled) then return end
if _G.Jewski_IsResetting then return end
_reviveHum(hum)
end))
local _lastHarden = 0
_arHardenConn = _regConn(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not (_G.AntiRagdollEnabled or _G.antiKnockbackEnabled) then return end
if _G.Jewski_IsResetting then return end
if not hum or not hum.Parent then return end
local now = os.clock()
if now - _lastHarden >= 1 then
_lastHarden = now
_hardHum(hum)
end
if hum.Health <= 0 then _reviveHum(hum); return end
local st = hum:GetState()
if st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead
or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics then
pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) end)
end
end))
end
local function isFlyingCarpetActive()
if not antiRagdollCharacter then return false end
local hrp = antiRagdollCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if hrp then
for _, obj in ipairs(hrp:GetChildren()) do
if obj:IsA("BodyVelocity") or obj:IsA("BodyPosition") or obj:IsA("BodyGyro") then
return true
end
end
end
return false
end
local function isRagdolled()
if not antiRagdollHumanoid then return false end
local state = antiRagdollHumanoid:GetState()
return state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp
end
local function enableAntiRagdollControls()
pcall(function()
local PlayerModule = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerScripts"):WaitForChild("PlayerModule", 10)
require(PlayerModule):GetControls():Enable()
end)
end
local function cleanupRagdoll()
if not antiRagdollCharacter then return end
local carpetEquipped = isFlyingCarpetActive()
local function processChildren(parent)
for _, obj in ipairs(parent:GetChildren()) do
if obj:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") or obj:IsA("NoCollisionConstraint") or obj:IsA("HingeConstraint")
or (obj:IsA("Attachment") and (obj.Name == "A" or obj.Name == "B")) then
obj:Destroy()
elseif obj:IsA("BodyVelocity") or obj:IsA("BodyPosition") or obj:IsA("BodyGyro") then
if not carpetEquipped then obj:Destroy() end
elseif obj:IsA("Motor6D") then
obj.Enabled = true
elseif obj:IsA("BasePart") then
for _, child in ipairs(obj:GetChildren()) do
if child:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") or child:IsA("NoCollisionConstraint")
or child:IsA("HingeConstraint") or child:IsA("Motor6D") then
if child:IsA("Motor6D") then child.Enabled = true
else child:Destroy() end
elseif child:IsA("Attachment") and (child.Name == "A" or child.Name == "B") then
child:Destroy()
end
end
end
end
end
pcall(function() processChildren(antiRagdollCharacter) end)
if antiRagdollAnimator then
for _, track in pairs(antiRagdollAnimator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
local animName = track.Animation and track.Animation.Name:lower() or ""
if animName:find("rag") or animName:find("fall") or animName:find("hurt") or animName:find("down") then
track:Stop(0)
end
end
end
end
local function setupAntiRagdollCharacter(char)
antiRagdollCharacter = char
antiRagdollHumanoid  = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
antiRagdollRootPart  = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)
antiRagdollAnimator  = antiRagdollHumanoid and antiRagdollHumanoid:WaitForChild("Animator", 10)
lastVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
end
local function clearAntiRagdollConnections()
for _, c in pairs(antiRagdollConnections) do
pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
end
antiRagdollConnections = {}
end
local function setupAntiRagdollConnections()
clearAntiRagdollConnections()
if not antiRagdollHumanoid or not antiRagdollRootPart then return end
antiRagdollConnections[#antiRagdollConnections+1] = _regConn(
antiRagdollHumanoid.StateChanged:Connect(function()
if not (_G.AntiRagdollEnabled or _G.antiKnockbackEnabled) then return end
if isRagdolled() then
if not isFlyingCarpetActive() then
antiRagdollHumanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
end
cleanupRagdoll()
pcall(function() Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = antiRagdollHumanoid end)
enableAntiRagdollControls()
end
end))
pcall(function()
local net = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Packages")
if net then net = net:FindFirstChild("Net") end
if net then net = net:FindFirstChild("RE/CombatService/ApplyImpulse") end
if net then
antiRagdollConnections[#antiRagdollConnections+1] = _regConn(
net.OnClientEvent:Connect(function()
if (_G.AntiRagdollEnabled or _G.antiKnockbackEnabled) and isRagdolled() then
antiRagdollRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
end
end))
end
end)
antiRagdollConnections[#antiRagdollConnections+1] = _regConn(
antiRagdollCharacter.DescendantAdded:Connect(function()
if (_G.AntiRagdollEnabled or _G.antiKnockbackEnabled) and isRagdolled() then
cleanupRagdoll()
end
end))
antiRagdollConnections[#antiRagdollConnections+1] = _regConn(
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not (_G.AntiRagdollEnabled or _G.antiKnockbackEnabled) then return end
if not isRagdolled() then return end
cleanupRagdoll()
local velocity = antiRagdollRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity
if (velocity - lastVelocity).Magnitude > velocityChangeThreshold
and velocity.Magnitude > velocityMagnitudeThreshold then
antiRagdollRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity =
velocity.Unit * math.min(velocity.Magnitude, maxVelocity)
end
lastVelocity = velocity
end))
enableAntiRagdollControls()
cleanupRagdoll()
end
function startAntiRagdoll()
_G.AntiRagdollEnabled   = true
_G.antiKnockbackEnabled = true
if Config then Config.AntiRagdoll = true end
pcall(function() if type(SaveConfig) == "function" then SaveConfig() end end)
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
if char then
local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if hum then _hardHum(hum) end
_bindHardener(char)
setupAntiRagdollCharacter(char)
setupAntiRagdollConnections()
end
end
function stopAntiRagdoll()
_G.AntiRagdollEnabled   = false
_G.antiKnockbackEnabled = false
if Config then Config.AntiRagdoll = false end
pcall(function() if type(SaveConfig) == "function" then SaveConfig() end end)
if _arHardenConn then pcall(function() _arHardenConn:Disconnect() end); _arHardenConn = nil end
if _arHealthConn then pcall(function() _arHealthConn:Disconnect() end); _arHealthConn = nil end
if _arDiedConn   then pcall(function() _arDiedConn:Disconnect()   end); _arDiedConn   = nil end
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
local hum  = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if hum then _softHum(hum) end
clearAntiRagdollConnections()
end
_G.toggleAntiRagdoll    = function(en) if en then startAntiRagdoll() else stopAntiRagdoll() end end
_G.enableAntiKnockback  = function() startAntiRagdoll() end
_G.disableAntiKnockback = function() stopAntiRagdoll() end
_regConn(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
clearAntiRagdollConnections()
antiRagdollCharacter = nil; antiRagdollHumanoid = nil
antiRagdollRootPart  = nil; antiRagdollAnimator = nil
local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
local hrp = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)
if not hum or not hrp then return end
task.wait(0.2)
if _G.AntiRagdollEnabled or _G.antiKnockbackEnabled then
_hardHum(hum)
_bindHardener(char)
setupAntiRagdollCharacter(char)
setupAntiRagdollConnections()
end
end))
if LocalPlayer.Character then
local hum = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if hum and (_G.AntiRagdollEnabled or _G.antiKnockbackEnabled) then
_hardHum(hum)
end
_bindHardener(LocalPlayer.Character)
setupAntiRagdollCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character)
if _G.AntiRagdollEnabled or _G.antiKnockbackEnabled then
setupAntiRagdollConnections()
end
end
if Config and Config.AntiRagdoll then
task.defer(function() pcall(startAntiRagdoll) end)
end
end)()
;(function()
local RunSvc = game:GetService("RunService")
UIS = UIS or game:GetService("UserInputService")
LP = LP or game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
Config = Config or _G.JewskiUIConfig or {}
_G.JewskiUIConfig = Config
ShowNotification = ShowNotification or function(_, _) end
if not _G._JEWSKI_InfJumpState then
_G._JEWSKI_InfJumpState = { enabled = false, conn = nil, lastJump = 0 }
end
local IJ = _G._JEWSKI_InfJumpState
local function _infJumpSet(en)
IJ.enabled = en
if Config then Config.InfiniteJump = en end
pcall(SaveConfig)
if IJ.conn then pcall(function() IJ.conn:Disconnect() end); IJ.conn = nil end
if not en then return end
IJ.conn = RunSvc.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not IJ.enabled then return end
if not UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then return end
local now = tick()
if now - IJ.lastJump < 0.1 then return end
local c = LP.Character
if not c then return end
local hrp = c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local hum = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not hrp or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
IJ.lastJump = now
hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(
hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, 55, hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z)
end)
end
_G.setInfiniteJump   = _infJumpSet
_G.Jewski_SetInfJump    = _infJumpSet
_G.Jewski_ToggleInfJump = function()
_infJumpSet(not IJ.enabled)
ShowNotification("INF JUMP", IJ.enabled and "ON" or "OFF")
end
_G.Jewski_GetInfJumpEnabled = function() return IJ.enabled end
if Config and Config.InfiniteJump then
task.defer(function() pcall(_infJumpSet, true) end)
end
end)()
;
task.spawn(function()
local LocalPlayer = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
local PlayerGui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 10)
if not PlayerGui then return end
local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local old = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("JewskiKeybindsPanel")
if old then old:Destroy() end
local ACCENT = Color3.fromRGB(90, 140, 255)
local BG = Color3.fromRGB(14, 15, 22)
local ROW = Color3.fromRGB(20, 21, 30)
local TEXT = Color3.fromRGB(235, 238, 248)
local DIM = Color3.fromRGB(130, 135, 165)
local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
gui.Name = "JewskiKeybindsPanel"
gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
gui.DisplayOrder = 130
gui.Parent = (gethui and gethui()) or PlayerGui
local outer = Instance.new("Frame", gui)
outer.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(260, 400)
outer.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(340, 120)
outer.BackgroundColor3 = BG
outer.BorderSizePixel = 0
outer.Visible = false
outer.Active = true
Instance.new("UICorner", outer).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)
local ost = Instance.new("UIStroke", outer)
ost.Color = ACCENT
ost.Thickness = 1
ost.Transparency = 0.4
local header = Instance.new("TextButton", outer)
header.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34)
header.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 19, 28)
header.Text = "  KEYBINDS"
header.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
header.TextSize = 13
header.TextColor3 = TEXT
header.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
header.AutoButtonColor = false
header.BorderSizePixel = 0
Instance.new("UICorner", header).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)
do
local dragging, dragStart, startPos = false, nil, nil
header.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
dragging = true
dragStart = input.Position
startPos = outer.Position
end
end)
UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(inp)
if not dragging then return end
if inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
local d = inp.Position - dragStart
outer.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(startPos.X.Offset + d.X, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
end)
UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(inp)
if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
dragging = false
end
end)
end
local closeBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", header)
closeBtn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 26)
closeBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -30, 0, 4)
closeBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
closeBtn.Text = "X"
closeBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
closeBtn.TextSize = 13
closeBtn.TextColor3 = DIM
closeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() outer.Visible = false end)
local scroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame", outer)
scroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -42)

local function currentKey()
local v = _G[globalName]
return (type(v) == "string" and v ~= "") and v or default
end
local function paint()
local k = currentKey()
btn.Text = k == "NONE" and "[NONE]" or ("[" .. k .. "]")
btn.BackgroundColor3 = k == "NONE" and Color3.fromRGB(30, 31, 42) or Color3.fromRGB(28, 40, 60)
btn.TextColor3 = k == "NONE" and DIM or ACCENT
end
paint()
local listening = false
local conn
btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
if listening then return end
listening = true
btn.Text = "..."
conn = UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp, gp)
if gp then return end
if inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
local kn = inp.KeyCode.Name
if kn == "Unknown" then return end
_G[globalName] = kn
pcall(function()
if type(_G.JewskiSaveConfigNow) == "function" then _G.JewskiSaveConfigNow()
elseif type(_G.JewskiSaveConfig) == "function" then _G.JewskiSaveConfig() end
end)
paint()
listening = false
if conn then conn:Disconnect(); conn = nil end
end)
end)
clearBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
if listening and conn then conn:Disconnect(); conn = nil end
listening = false
_G[globalName] = "NONE"
pcall(function()
if type(_G.JewskiSaveConfigNow) == "function" then _G.JewskiSaveConfigNow()
elseif type(_G.JewskiSaveConfig) == "function" then _G.JewskiSaveConfig() end
end)
paint()
end)
end

do
local function _jewskiInstantClone()
	if _G.isCloning and (not _G._cloneLockAt or (os.clock() - _G._cloneLockAt) > 1.0) then
		_G.isCloning = false
	end
	if _G.isCloning then return false end
	local Players = game:GetService("Players")
	local LP = Players.LocalPlayer
	local c = LP.Character
	if not c then return false end
	local h = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not h then return false end
	local bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
	local cl = (bp and bp:FindFirstChild("Quantum Cloner")) or c:FindFirstChild("Quantum Cloner")
	if not cl then return false end
	pcall(function() h:UnequipTools() end)
	task.wait()
	if cl.Parent ~= c then
		pcall(function() h:EquipTool(cl) end)
		task.wait()
	end
	local pg = LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
	local tf = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("ToolsFrames")
	local qc = tf and tf:FindFirstChild("QuantumCloner")
	local tb = qc and qc:FindFirstChild("TeleportToClone")
	_G.isCloning = true
	_G._cloneLockAt = os.clock()
	pcall(function() cl:Activate() end)
	task.wait(0.05)
	if tb and type(firesignal) == "function" then
		pcall(function() tb.Visible = true end)
		pcall(function() firesignal(tb.MouseButton1Click) end)
		pcall(function() firesignal(tb.MouseButton1Up) end)
		pcall(function() firesignal(tb.Activated) end)
	end
	task.delay(0.35, function() _G.isCloning = false end)
	return true
end
_G.JewskiInstantClone = _jewskiInstantClone
_G.instantClone = _jewskiInstantClone
_G.JewskiInstantClone = _jewskiInstantClone
instantClone = _jewskiInstantClone
end

task.spawn(function()
local LP = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
local function isBad(obj)
if not obj then return false end
local n = tostring(obj.Name or ""):lower()
if n:find("jewski", 1, true) or n:find("jewskiextras", 1, true) then return true end
if n == "extras" or n == "open gui" then return true end
if n:find("stealtarget", 1, true) or n:find("steal_target", 1, true) then return true end
if n:find("autosteal", 1, true) and n:find("jewski", 1, true) then return true end
if n == "steal target" or n == "stealtargetgui" or n == "jewskistealtarget" then return true end
if obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton") then
local tx = tostring(obj.Text or ""):lower()
if tx:find("jewski", 1, true) or tx:find("discord.gg/jewski", 1, true) then return true end
end
if obj:IsA("ScreenGui") then
if n:find("jewski", 1, true) then return true end
if n:find("stealtarget", 1, true) and not n:find("jewski", 1, true) and not n:find("anglvs", 1, true) then
return true
end
end
return false
end
local function hook(root)
if not root or root:GetAttribute("__JEWSKI_NoMark") then return end
root:SetAttribute("__JEWSKI_NoMark", true)
root.ChildAdded:Connect(function(ch)
task.defer(function()
if isBad(ch) then _G.Jewski_ParkOffscreen(ch); return end
pcall(function()
for _, d in ipairs(ch:GetDescendants()) do
if isBad(d) then _G.Jewski_ParkOffscreen(d) end
end
end)
end)
end)
end
local pg = LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
if pg then hook(pg) end
LP.ChildAdded:Connect(function(ch)
if ch:IsA("PlayerGui") then hook(ch) end
end)
pcall(function()
if gethui then hook(gethui()) end
end)
end)

	local function _fpsNuke()
		pcall(function()
			if setfpscap then setfpscap(240) end
			settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = Enum.QualityLevel.Level01
			local L = game:GetService("Lighting")
			L.GlobalShadows = false
			L.FogEnd = 9e9
			for _, v in ipairs(L:GetDescendants()) do
				if v:IsA("PostEffect") then pcall(function() v.Enabled = false end) end
			end
		end)
	end
	_G.Jewski_SetFPSBoost = function(on)
		_G.JewskiFpsBoost = on ~= false
		if _G.JewskiFpsBoost then _fpsNuke() end
	end
	task.spawn(function()
		_fpsNuke()
		while true do
			task.wait(6)
			if _G.JewskiFpsBoost ~= false then pcall(_fpsNuke) end
		end
	end)
end
